> Ntụaka zuru ezu maka environment variable ọ bụla OmniRoute na-amata.
> Maka template mbido-ngwa-ngwa, lee [`.env.example`](../../.env.example).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Variable ọ bụla edepụtara ebe a ga-apụtakwa na `.env.example`, variable ọ bụla dị na
> `.env.example` ga-apụtakwa ebe a. `npm run check:env-doc-sync`
> na-amanye nke a mgbe a na-eme commit nakwa na CI. Iji kpachapụ variable ụma, tinye ya na
> allowlist dị n'ime `scripts/check/check-env-doc-sync.mjs`.

## 1. Ihe Nzuzo Ndị A Chọrọ

A **ga-**ahazi ihe ndị a tupu mmalite mbụ. Na-enweghị ha, ngwa ahụ ga-ajụ ịmalite ma ọ bụ jiri ntọala ndabara na-enweghị nchebe rụọ ọrụ.

| Ihe Mgbanwe                  | Achọrọ Ya        | Ndabara                          | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------------------- | ---------------- | -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `JWT_SECRET`                 | **Ee**           | _(ọ dịghị)_                      | `src/lib/auth`                                            | Na-eji mbinye aka echekwa/nyochaa kuki nnọkọ dashboard niile (JWT). Jiri `openssl rand -base64 48` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `API_KEY_SECRET`             | **Ee**           | _(ọ dịghị)_                      | `src/lib/db/apiKeys.ts`                                   | Igodo nzuzo AES maka ụkpụrụ igodo API echekwara na SQLite. Jiri `openssl rand -hex 32` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `INITIAL_PASSWORD`           | **Ee**           | `CHANGEME`                       | Skripụ mbido                                              | Na-edobe okwuntughe mbụ nke dashboard onye nchịkwa (ọ dabara na ndabara `.env.example` — e mere ka o doo anya na ọ naghị echebe nke ọma iji manye ka a gbanwee ya). **Gbanwee ya tupu ojiji mbụ.** Mgbe ịbanyechara, gbanwee ya site na Dashboard → Settings → Security.                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET` | **Ee** (mmepụta) | _(edoghị ya)_                    | `src/app/api/internal/codex-responses-ws/route.ts`        | Nzuzo a na-ekekọrịta maka àkwà mmiri WebSocket nke Codex Responses dị n'ime. Ọ na-enyocha arịrịọ àkwà mmiri dị n'etiti nnyefe WS nke Electron/ihe nchọgharị na OmniRoute. ⚠️ **A CHỌRỌ YA na mmepụta — mgbe edoghị ya, a na-ajụ arịrịọ àkwà mmiri WS niile.** Jiri `openssl rand -base64 32` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SW_BUILD_ID`      | Mba              | _(git SHA)_                      | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | ID doro anya maka imebi cache nke service-worker maka shell PWA (#11779); ọ bụ nke mbụ n'usoro mkpebi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SOURCE_VERSION`             | Mba              | _(edoghị ya)_                    | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Nke abụọ n'usoro ahụ — ndị nrụpụta PaaS (dịka ụdị Heroku) na-edobe ya ka ọ bụrụ commit e bugoro.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SW_BUILD_ID`    | Mba              | _(e sitere na ndị ọzọ nweta ya)_ | `src/shared/components/PwaRegister.tsx`                   | Uru ọha nke oge nrụpụta nke klayent na-eji edebanye `/sw.js?v=…`; a na-enweta ya site na abụọ dị n'elu, emesịa site na git SHA.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_STAMP_TOKEN` | Mba (akpaka)     | _(akpaka n'oge mbido ọ bụla)_    | `src/server/authz/policies/management.ts`                 | Nzuzo nke usoro ọ bụla nke na-egosi na akara peer-IP a tụkwasịrị obi sitere na sava HTTP nke OmniRoute n'onwe ya (`scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`). Middleware authz na-atụkwasị obi na arịrịọ sitere na mpaghara ahụ (mgbochi loopback/LAN nke ụzọ LOCAL_ONLY) naanị mgbe akara ahụ nwere token a. A na-emepụta ya na-akpaghị aka n'oge mbido ọ bụla — hapụ ya ka edoghị ya; debe ya naanị maka nhazi nwere ọtụtụ usoro ndị ga-ekekọrịta akara ahụ. |

### Iwu Mmepụta

```bash
# Mepụta nzuzo anọ niile n'otu oge:
echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)"
echo "API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
echo "INITIAL_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 16)"
echo "OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

> [!CAUTION]
> Etinyela faịlụ `.env` nwere ezigbo nzuzo na njikwa ụdị. `.gitignore` ewepụla `.env`, mana nyochaa ya tupu iziga mgbanwe.

---

## 2. Nchekwa & Ebe Nchekwa Data

OmniRoute na-eji **SQLite** (site na `better-sqlite3`) maka nchekwa data niile na-adịgide adịgide. Mgbanwe ndị a na-achịkwa ebe data dị, izochi ya, na usoro ndụ ya.

| Mgbanwe                                    | Ndabara                       | Faịlụ Isi                                             | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `DATA_DIR`                                 | `~/.omniroute/`               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Akwụkwọ ndekọ mgbọrọgwụ maka SQLite DB, nkwado ndabere, na faịlụ data. Gbanwee ya maka volume Docker ma ọ bụ ụzọ ahaziri iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_DEFAULT_DATA_DIR`         | _(edoghị ya)_                 | `src/lib/dataPaths.ts`                                | Ụzọ mgbapụ maka ihe nche DATA_DIR nke ule/eval (#10428). A na-ebuga ule na nyocha eval/print nke Node (`-e`/`--eval`/`-p`/`--print`, gụnyere ụdị `--eval=`/`--print=`) ndị na-enweghị `DATA_DIR` gaa na akwụkwọ ndekọ temp a ga-atụfu atụfu ka ha ghara imepe ebe nchekwa data nke onye nchịkwa n'ezie; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji họrọ iji ezigbo akwụkwọ ndekọ ahụ ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_SHA`                      | _(edoghị ya)_                 | `src/lib/monitoring/buildSha.ts`                      | Git SHA nke artifact na-arụ ọrụ. `npm run build:release` na-akanye ya; enwere ike itinye ya n'ime container ndị na-enweghị akara `dist/BUILD_SHA`. A na-egosi ya dị ka `system.buildSha` na `/api/monitoring/health`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELEASE_REF`                    | `origin/main`                 | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Ref nke mgbochi provenance nke pack-artifact ji atụnyere build SHA (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CANARY_BUILD`             | _(edoghị ya)_                 | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji kwe ka a kwakọba build nke SHA ya na-anọghị n'ahịrị release, na-edekọ ya dị ka canary e bu n'obi kama ime ka mgbochi ahụ daa (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_API_KEY`                  | _(edoghị ya)_                 | `scripts/ops/deploy-canary.mjs`                       | API key maka nyocha smoke nke mbugharị canary, nke a na-eziga dị ka `Authorization: Bearer` na `/v1/chat/completions`. Naanị script mbugharị na-eji ya (#10429), sava anaghị eji ya ma ọlị. Ọ naghị emetụta mgbanwe `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_*` nke usoro ule smoke CLI a ga-ahọrọ ịgbalite (`RUN_CLI_SMOKE=1`, `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_BASE_URL/MODEL/API_KEY_ENV/TARGETS/TIMEOUT_MS` dị na `tests/integration/upstream-cli-smoke.int.test.ts`) — lee [Njikọ CLI → Nnyocha smoke n'ezie](../guides/CLI-INTEGRATIONS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILDING`                       | _(edoghị ya)_                 | `src/lib/buildPhase.ts`                               | Akara oge build (#10060): `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs` na-edobe ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`, worker build ọ bụla e mepụtara na-eketakwa ya, ka layer DB wee weghachite stub na-adịghị eme ihe kama ibunye addon native better-sqlite3 (nke na-eme ka worker kwụsị mgbe ọ na-apụ). Atọla ya mgbe sava na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_NATIVE_DEP_CHECK`          | `0`                           | `scripts/check/check-native-deps.mjs`                 | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji mafere nyocha prebuild nke native-dependency nhọrọ maka osisi vendored pụrụ iche. Nke a anaghị eme ka dependency na-efu efu nwee ike ịrụ build; jiri ya naanị mgbe a na-eweta native dependencies site n'ụzọ ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`                       | _(edoghị ya)_                 | `open-sse/executors/promptql/threadSticky.ts`         | **Aha nnọchi fallback** maka `DATA_DIR`, nke a na-enyocha naanị mgbe edoghị `DATA_DIR`. A na-eji ya achọta cache session thread-sticky nke executor PromptQL dị na diski (`<dir>/promptql-thread-sessions.json`); ọ bụrụ na edoghị nke ọ bụla n'ime mgbanwe abụọ ahụ, cache ahụ ga-anọ naanị na ebe nchekwa (a gaghị echekwa ya mgbe emalitegharịrị usoro ahụ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR`                    | _(edoghị ya)_                 | `src/lib/plugins/scanner.ts`                          | Akwụkwọ ndekọ nke **runtime plugin scanner** na-agụ — nakwa mgbọrọgwụ ebe plugin manager na-etinye ihe — nke na-anọchi ndabara sitere na home (#11827). Tụọ ya aka na osisi plugin e ji bind mount tinye na Docker/K8s kama ịkwaga HOME naanị iji gbanwee ebe ụzọ nyocha dị (HOME na-achịkwa omume ndị ọzọ niile dabere na home kwa). Mgbe edoghị ya = `~/.omniroute/plugins`, ma ọ bụ `/tmp/.omniroute/plugins` mgbe process ahụ anaghị ebupụ home ma ọlị — nke a bụ nsogbu enweghị nchọpụta na-enweghị ọkwa nke mgbanwe a na-ewepụ. A na-edekọ akwụkwọ ndekọ ahụ e kpebiri otu ugboro mgbe mmalite dị ka `scanner.dir_resolved`, tinyere input nke meriri. Maka akụkụ sava naanị: plugin command CLI na-edobe `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH` nke ha (ngalaba 9). |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`                   | _(oghere efu = agbanyụghị)_   | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | Igodo AES maka izochi nchekwa data SQLite niile mgbe echekwara ya. Jiri `openssl rand -hex 32` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY_VERSION`           | `v1`                          | `scripts/build/bootstrap-env.mjs`, `electron/main.js` | Akara ụdị maka igodo izochi. Bulie ya mgbe a na-agbanwe igodo ka e nwee ike ịkpọghe nkwado ndabere ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`               | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/backup.ts`                                | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, ọ na-amafe nkwado ndabere faịlụ SQLite a na-eme mgbe niile/tupu ide ihe (ichekwa/ihichapụ ọnụahịa models.dev, ide ntọala). Nkwado ndabere ejiri aka mee na nke a na-eme tupu iweghachi ka na-arụ ọrụ. Ọ **naghị** agbanyụ snapshot nchekwa na-adịgide adịgide nke onye na-agba migration chọrọ n'ike, ma ọ bụ mgbochi mass-migration maka DB na-adịgide adịgide dịbu adị. A na-amachi nkwado ndabere ndị na-abụghị nke ejiri aka mee ka ha ghara ịkarị otu ugboro n'ime nkeji 60. **Settings → Storage** dị na dashboard nwere ike ịgbanyụ nkwado ndabere akpaaka a na-eme mgbe niile n'onwe ya.                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CRYPT_KEY`                      | _(edoghị)_                    | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Aha nnọchi ochie** maka `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`. A na-anabata ya dị ka nhọrọ ndabere mgbe isi variable ahụ adịghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_BASE64`                 | _(edoghị)_                    | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Aha nnọchi ochie** (ụdị e ji Base64 koodu mee) a na-anabata dị ka nhọrọ ndabere. A na-akpọghe koodu ya na-akpaghị aka tupu eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_DB_HEALTHCHECK_INTERVAL_MS`     | _(edoghị)_                    | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Gbanwee ogologo oge dị n'etiti nyocha ahụike SQLite oge ọbụla (ms). Mgbe edoghị ya, a na-enweta ndabara site na `NODE_ENV`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`       | _(ewepụla)_                   | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | **Ewepụla ya.** `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` a na-eme mgbe niile mgbe sistemụ na-arụ ọrụ nwere ike imebi mapping wal-index a na-ekekọrịta ma mee ka process ahụ daa site na SIGBUS (#13973), ya mere scheduler ahụ adịghịzi. Variable ahụ anaghịzi arụ ọrụ: uru dị mma na-edekọ ịdọ aka ná ntị otu ugboro na-egosi na a kwụsịla iji ya, ebe `0` ma ọ bụ ọnọdụ edoghị ya na-anọ jụụ. A na-elekọta WAL site na checkpoint PASSIVE (n'okpuru), a na-ebipụkwa ya site na checkpoint mgbe a na-emechi sistemụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_PASSIVE_INTERVAL_MS`        | `300000` (nkeji 5)            | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Gbanwee ogologo oge dị n'etiti `wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE)` ndị a na-eme ugboro ugboro (ms). Nke a na-eme ka frame WAL ndị na-echere dị ntakịrị, ka checkpoint nọgide na-adị ngwa, meekwa ka nha faịlụ WAL ghara ịgafe oke n'etiti mbepụ ndị a na-eme mgbe a na-emechi sistemụ. `0` na-agbanyụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_GUARD_MAX_MB`               | `256`                         | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Mgbe tick PASSIVE chọpụtara na faịlụ WAL akarịala nha a, gbaa `wal_checkpoint(RESTART)` ka WAL malitegharịa n'edegharịghị wal-index e mere mapping ya. Ewepụla checkpoint ndị na-eji truncate-mode mgbe sistemụ na-arụ ọrụ (lee ahịrị `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji kwụsị process ahụ mgbe nrụgide resource dị oke njọ gara n'ihu ruo `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS`, ka supervisor (systemd `Restart=always`, iwu restart Docker) nwee ike iweghachi process dị ọcha kama ịga n'ihu na-enye 503 ruo mgbe ebighị ebi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS` | `120000` (nkeji 2)            | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Ogologo oge nrụgide dị oke njọ ga-adịgide tupu nkwụsị nke na-akpalite restart nke onwe ya amalite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_PSI_DISABLED`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressurePolicy.ts`            | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji leghara `/proc/pressure/memory` (`psi_some` / `psi_full`) nke host niile anya mgbe onye agbata obi na-eme oke mkpọtụ na-akpalite 503 ebe process a dị mma (#13124). Ihe nche cgroup na V8 heap ka na-arụ ọrụ. PSI ga-akwụsịkwa ịgụ dị ka ihe akaebe kernel throttle maka `cgroup_high`: ngụkọta cgroup karịrị `memory.high` ga-abụzi nke dị oke njọ naanị ma ọ bụrụ na `high`/`max` dị na `memory.events` abawanye. Ọ bụrụ na `memory.events` adịghị, a ga-edobe raw ratio ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLJS_WASM_PATH`                | _(nchọpụta akpaaka)_          | `src/lib/db/adapters/sqljsAdapter.ts`                 | Path akọwapụtara (zuru ezu ma ọ bụ dabere na cwd) gaa na `sql-wasm.wasm` mgbe a na-eji adapter ndabere WASM nke `sql.js`. Mgbe edoghị ya, a na-achọpụta ya na-akpaghị aka site na dependency nke package na nhazi ndị nwere ike ịbụ nhọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`           | `30`                          | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Ọnụọgụ ụbọchị a ga-edobe checkpoint, faịlụ input/output/error ndị e zoro aka na ha, na row nke ọrụ Batch API ruru njedebe (completed/failed/cancelled/expired) tupu ihichapụ ha site na mkpocha akpaaka. Ọ na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ naanị mgbe agbanyere `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`; ọ dabara na oge OpenAI ji edobe output Batch API nke ya. Ọ naghị emetụta route `DELETE /api/v1/batches/delete-completed` nke operator na-akpalite, nke na-anọgide na-enweghị ọnọdụ ọ bụla (enweghị nzacha afọ) dịka e si hazie ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`      | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, kwe ka nhichapụ akpaka hichapụ ọrụ Batch API ndị ruru ọnọdụ ikpeazụ (yana ebe nchekwa ọganihu ha) gafere `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`, ma hichapụ ọdịnaya BLOB nke faịlụ ndị ebugoro mgbe ha gafere `expires_at` nke ha. A na-agbanyụ ya na ndabara: nrụnye niile dị ugbu a ga-edobe data a otu ọ dị na mbụ ruo mgbe onye nchịkwa họọrọ ịgbanye ya. Ọ bụkwa ọkọlọtọ njirimara enwere ike idezi na dashboard — lee `docs/reference/FEATURE_FLAGS.md` → Oge ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`            | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`, `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji mafe nyocha ahụike DB kpamkpam mgbe usoro na-amalite. Ọ bara uru maka ọrụ ndị na-adịru obere oge na ule njikọta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORCE_DB_HEALTHCHECK`           | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji manye loop nyocha ahụike DB ka ọ rụọ ọrụ, ọbụna mgbe a ga-amafe ya n’ọnọdụ nkịtị (dịka ọmụmaatụ, ọrụ ndị na-adịru obere oge).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_POSTINSTALL`               | `0`                           | `scripts/postinstall.mjs`                             | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji mafe nkwadebe native-runtime n’oge `npm install`. Ọ bara uru na nrụnye CI/headless ebe arụpụtalarị sqlite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_MIGRATIONS_DIR`                 | _(chọpụta na-akpaghị aka)_    | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Dochie ndekọ nke migration runner na-enyocha. Ọ bara uru mgbe a na-ebunye migration ndị agbakwụnyere ọnụ n’ime build omenala.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_EXTRA_MIGRATIONS_DIRS`          | _(edobeghị)_                  | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner/extraDirs.ts`             | Ndekọ migration ndị ọzọ dịka ntinye `namespace=dir` nke ihe nkewa path nke platform kewara (dịka `ee=/opt/app/enterprise/db/migrations`). A na-edekọ faịlụ ndị achọtara ebe ahụ dịka `<namespace>-<number>`, ya mere nkesa na-ebunye migration nke ya agaghị emegide oghere ọnụọgụ nke upstream. Ntinye e mebiri emebi, namespace na-adịghị irè, ma ọ bụ ndekọ na-adịghị adị ga-ebute njehie mgbe usoro na-amalite kama ịmafe schema ahụ n’enweghị ọkwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_PENDING_MIGRATIONS`         | `50`                          | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Oke nchekwa maka ọtụtụ migration na-echere (#3416). Mmalite usoro ga-akwụsị ma ọ bụrụ na migration ndị na-echere karịrị ọnụọgụ a na DB dị ugbu a (nke a na-echebe megide tebụl nsochi e hichapụrụ). Welie ya iji weghachite backup ochie; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ nyocha ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTALL_UPGRADE_WORKDIR`        | _(`<repo>/.install-upgrade`)_ | `scripts/check/check-install-upgrade.mjs`             | Ndekọ ọrụ maka ọnụ ụzọ mwepụta `check:install-upgrade`. Ọ chọrọ ihe dịka 12 GB (osisi nrụnye abụọ nke ihe dịka 3 GB nke ọ bụla tinyere tarball), ya mere, ọ gaghị agba ọsọ na tmpfs pere mpe — na runner nke a na-akwado n’onwe ya, `/tmp` bụ tmpfs 12 GB nke RAM na-akwado, ọnụ ụzọ ahụ mejupụtara ya, wee bepụ package ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS`        | _(ndabara dị na koodu)_       | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Oge dị n’etiti flush (ms) maka onye na-ede spend/cost n’ìgwè. Uru ndị dị ala na-ebelata ijikọta write; uru ndị dị elu na-ebelata esemokwu DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_MAX_BUFFER_SIZE`          | _(ndabara dị na koodu)_       | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Ọnụọgụ ntinye spend kachasị enwere ike idobe na buffer tupu e manye flush. Welie ya na deployment nwere QPS dị elu; wedata ya mgbe ebe nchekwa nwere oke dị mkpa karịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_FETCH_DEBUG`              | _(edobeghị)_                  | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                        | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `"true"` iji wepụta ndekọ debug `[ProxyFetch]` n’ụzọ relay Vercel. A na-agbanyụ ya na ndabara iji zere ikpughe ntụaka routing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                    | `false`                       | `src/lib/proxyLogger.ts`                              | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `"true"` ma ọ bụ `"1"` iji tinye IP ndị client/egress na prefix nke account n’ahịrị ndekọ usoro `[ProxyEgress]` zuru ezu. A na-edobe ya ka ọ bụrụ OFF na ndabara ka ndekọ usoro ghara ikpughe IP ma ọ bụ prefix nke account.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG`                          | _(edobeghị)_                  | `bin/cli/commands/quota.mjs`                          | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji bipụta nchọpụta oge nke request ọ bụla (`[omniroute] GET <path> completed in Nms`) site na iwu quota CLI gaa na stderr.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_PATH`               | _(akpaka)_                    | `scripts/dev/healthcheck.mjs`                         | Path doro anya nke nyocha ahụike container na-enyocha. Mgbe edobeghị ya, probe na-enweta ya site na `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`; ịtọ ya na-eme ka a laghachi n’iji endpoint nlekota miri emi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG_COMPLETION`               | _(edobeghị)_                  | `bin/cli/commands/completion.mjs`                     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ uru ọ bụla na-abụghị ihe efu iji wepụta nchọpụta `[omniroute completion]` site na path cache shell-completion nke CLI (gụ/mee ka ọ dị ọhụrụ/dee). A na-agbanyụ ya na ndabara — cache ndị ahụ na-ada n’enweghị ọkwa ka cache na-efu efu ma ọ bụ mebiri emebi ghara imebi tab-completion.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `BATCH_RETRY_DURATION_MS`                  | `86400000` (24h)              | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Oke oge a ga-eji nwaa ọzọ maka ihe ọ bụla dị na batch (ms). A ga-akara ihe gafere oge a dị ka nke dara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_BASE_MS`                    | `5000`                        | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Oge nchere ntọala (ms) maka exponential backoff mgbe a na-anwa ihe dị na batch ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_MAX_MS`                     | `3600000` (1h)                | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Oke kachasị (ms) maka exponential backoff n'etiti nnwale ọzọ nke ihe ndị dị na batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `BATCH_MAX_CONCURRENT`                     | `1`                           | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Ọnụọgụ batch kachasị a ga-ahazi n'otu oge. Bulie ya iji mee ka ọrụ na-aga ngwa ngwa; debe ya ala iji zere ọtụtụ arịrịọ ga-agafe oke ọnụego.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Tupu ịgbanwe nchekwa data na-adịgide adịgide nke dịbu, migration runner na-ebipụta snapshot zuru ezu,
> nke e ji ọdịnaya ya mata, n'okpuru `DATA_DIR/db_backups/`. Mbipụta chọrọ filesystem
> nke na-akwado hard link ndị nọ n'otu filesystem, anaghị edegharị faịlụ dịbu, yana file sync na-adịgide adịgide. Host POSIX chọkwara
> directory sync; na Windows, Node nwere ike ịjụ directory handle, ya mere OmniRoute na-eme flush nke
> faịlụ e bipụtara ma na-ewere directory-entry sync dị ka mgbalị kachasị mma.
> Ọ bụrụ na `DATA_DIR` e mount-ere enweghị ike inye nkwa ndị ahụ, startup ga-akwụsị n'ụzọ nchekwa tupu o tinye
> migration. Bugharịa `DATA_DIR` gaa na volume nwere ikike ndị ahụ; ejila
> `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP` gafere nchekwa migration.

### Ọnọdụ ojiji

| Ọnọdụ ojiji                  | Nhazi                                                                          |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| **Mmepe n'igwe mpaghara**    | Hapụ ndabara niile. DB dị na `~/.omniroute/omniroute.db`.                      |
| **Docker**                   | `DATA_DIR=/data` + mount volume na `/data`.                                    |
| **Ezoro ezo mgbe echekwara** | Tọọ `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + debe backup nke key ahụ! Ịtụfu ya = ịtụfu data. |
| **CI/Nnwale**                | `DATA_DIR=/tmp/omniroute-test` — ọ bụ nwa oge, encryption adịghị mkpa.         |

---

## 3. Netwọk na Pọtị

| Mgbanwe                                     | Ndabara                                    | Faịlụ Mmalite                                                            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PORT`                                      | `20128`                                    | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Pọtị bụ́ isi maka Dashboard UI na njedebe API niile (ọnọdụ otu pọtị).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`                       | _(oghere = mgbọrọgwụ)_                     | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/docker/ensure-docker-base-path.mjs`          | Ụzọ nta URL maka ijere OmniRoute ozi n’azụ reverse proxy (na-ahazi `basePath` nke Next.js; ntụgharị ụzọ nyocha njirimara na-eburu basePath n’uche). Dịka ọmụmaatụ, `/omniroute`. Na Docker, a na-etinye uru ahụ n’ime ya n’oge `docker build` (`ARG OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`); onyonyo mgbọrọgwụ e wurula nwere ike itinye uru runtime dị iche otu ugboro mgbe container malitere, tupu Next.js ebido. Hazie `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` ka ọ bụrụ origin ọha nke gụnyere otu ụzọ nta ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`           | _(oghere = mgbọrọgwụ)_                     | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                  | Oyiri `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` nke ihe nchọgharị na-ahụ, nke a na-etinye ozugbo n’oge build ka ngosipụta njedebe dashboard gosi `https://host/omniroute/v1` kama `https://host/v1`. Ọ na-eji `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` ma ọ bụrụ na ahazighị ya. Wughachi ya mgbe ị gbanwere ya (`basePath` nke Next bụ nke oge build).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED`                     | _(ahazighị = enweghị ike itinye na frame)_ | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/dashboardEmbed.mjs`                    | Nhọrọ nkwenye maka itinye ibe HTML n’ime iframe. Mgbe ahazighị ya, route ọ bụla na-eziga `frame-ancestors 'none'` + `X-Frame-Options: DENY`. Hazie ya ka ọ bụrụ `vscode` iji jee ozi ibe ndị ahụ (dashboard, nbanye, dọkụmentị, ibe nnabata) site na `frame-ancestors 'self' vscode-webview:` na enweghị `X-Frame-Options`, ka VS Code Simple Browser nwee ike igosi ha (ọnọdụ `dashboardOpen: "editor"` nke OmniCopilot). Akụkụ API (`/api`, `/v1`, `/v1beta`, `/a2a`, `/healthz`, aha ọzọ ndị dị na ọkwa mgbọrọgwụ) na-edobe header ndị siri ike n’ọnọdụ ọ bụla. Naanị `vscode` ka a na-amata — `1`/`true` anaghị eme ka ọ rụọ ọrụ. Oge build: wughachi ya mgbe ị gbanwere ya (`docker build --build-arg DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED=vscode` maka onyonyo; ịhazi ya na nrụnye e wurula enweghị mmetụta ọ bụla).                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `API_PORT`                                  | _(ahazighị)_                               | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Mgbe ahaziri ya, ọ na-eje ozi proxy API `/v1/*` na pọtị a dị iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `API_HOST`                                  | `0.0.0.0`                                  | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Adreesị njikọ maka pọtị API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `DASHBOARD_PORT`                            | _(ahazighị)_                               | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Mgbe ahaziri ya, ọ na-eje ozi Dashboard UI na pọtị a dị iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNI_MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS`           | `0` _(agbanyụrụ)_                          | `src/sse/utils/backpressure.ts`                                          | Na-amachi njikọ chat ndị na-aga n’otu oge; arịrịọ gafere oke ahụ na-enweta `503` tinyere `Retry-After`. Integer dị mma na-eme ka nchedo ahụ rụọ ọrụ; enweghị nhazi/`0` na-agbanyụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTANCE_ID`                     | _(ahazighị)_                               | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | ID kwụsiri ike ma pụọ iche maka gateway a mgbe a na-ejikọta instance OmniRoute n’usoro. Ọ na-eme ka nlele peer-loop nke na-abata rụọ ọrụ. Mkpụrụedemede ndị a nabatara: mkpụrụedemede, ọnụọgụ, `.`, `_`, `:`, na `-`; kacha karịa mkpụrụedemede 64.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_URLS`                       | _(edobeghị ya)_                            | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`     | URL ntọala OmniRoute ndị e ji rikoma kewaa nke nwere ike ịnata `X-OmniRoute-Peer-Trace`. Naanị URL upstream ndị e tinyere kpọmkwem na ndepụta nnabata ga-enweta metadata peer; a naghị emetụ ndị na-eweta ọrụ ndị ọzọ niile aka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_MAX_HOPS`                   | `4`                                        | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Ọnụọgụ kacha elu nke instance OmniRoute ndị a garala na mbụ nke a na-anabata na arịrịọ e jikọtara n'usoro (`1`-`32`). Instance ndị e megharịrị ugboro ugboro ma ọ bụ mmefu oke enyere ga-eweghachite HTTP `508 Loop Detected`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT`                       | `20130`                                    | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port e bipụtara n'akụkụ host maka Dashboard na ọnọdụ production Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROD_API_PORT`                             | `20131`                                    | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port e bipụtara n'akụkụ host maka API na ọnọdụ production Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PORT`                            | _(edobeghị ya)_                            | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Ọ na-ebute ụzọ karịa `PORT` mgbe ọ na-arụ ọrụ n'ime Electron ma ọ bụ wrapper ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `LIVE_WS_PORT`                              | `20129`                                    | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Port maka sava nlekota ozugbo nke WebSocket.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `LIVE_WS_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Adreesị bind maka sava WebSocket ozugbo. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0.0.0.0` iji mee ka a nweta ya na LAN (haziekwa `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                   | _(edobeghị ya)_                            | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Origin ndị ọzọ e ji rikoma kewaa nke e kwere ka ha mepee WebSocket ozugbo. E kweelarị origin dashboard loopback na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_HOSTS`                     | _(edobeghị ya)_                            | `src/server/ws/liveServerAllowList.ts`                                   | Aha host ndị ọzọ e ji rikoma kewaa nke e kwere maka origin WebSocket ozugbo. N'adịghị ka `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (URL origin zuru ezu), ọ na-atụnyere naanị akụkụ host — ọ bara uru maka nhazi LAN/Tailscale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_WS_PUBLIC_URL`            | _(edobeghị ya)_                            | `src/hooks/useLiveDashboard.ts`                                          | URL ọha maka WebSocket dashboard ozugbo (n'akụkụ ihe nchọgharị). Tọọ ya mgbe reverse proxy ma ọ bụ Cloudflare Tunnel nọ n'ihu sava WS (dịka `wss://ws.my-ai.com/live-ws`); ihe nchọgharị ahụ ga-ejikọta ebe ahụ kama `ws://hostname:20132`. A na-ejikwa akụkụ pathname dị ka ụzọ nkwalite WebSocket (ndabara: `/live-ws`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_LIVE_WS`                  | `true`                                     | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts` na `scripts/start-ws-server.mjs`           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` ma ọ bụ `false` iji gbanyụọ sava WebSocket ozugbo (agbanyere ya na ndabara, ma kee ya na loopback). Mgbanwe CI/harness nke na-agbanyụọ script enyemaka WebSocket ozugbo nke na-arụ ọrụ naanị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE`                       | `30`                                       | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/route.ts`                         | Oke ọsọ relay kwa (token, IP), arịrịọ/minit. Ọ dị na ebe nchekwa, kwa instance. `0` ma ọ bụ ọnụọgụ na-adịghị mma na-agbanyụọ ọnụ ụzọ nyocha nke akụkụ IP (oke DB kwa token ka na-emetụta).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `NODE_ENV`                                  | `production`                               | Isi Next.js                                                              | Na-achịkwa oke nkọwa ndekọ, caching, mkpughe nkọwa njehie, na nkwalite Next.js.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK`                   | `1` (Turbopack — ndabara koodu)            | `package.json` / Next.js 16                                              | Turbopack bụ bundler ndabara maka `npm run dev` na `npm run build` (ọ na-ewu ugboro 2-3× ọsọ ọsọ, dịka benchmark gosiri). Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji laghachi na webpack na Windows, mgbe ị zutere nsogbu ndakọrịta native binding / bundler, **ma ọ bụ na kọmputa RAM ya pere mpe** — a maara na upstream na mmepụta Turbopack na ahịrị ụdị Next.js a (16.2.x) na-eji ebe nchekwa dị elu karịa webpack nke ukwuu mgbe module graph buru ibu (ndozi memory-eviction nke Turbopack na Next 16.3 akabeghị kwụsie ike); ịlaghachi na webpack na-eji ebe nchekwa dị ala nke ukwuu. Lee #6409. **Mwube Docker image na-eji `0` (webpack) dịka ndabara**: `Dockerfile` na-ekwupụta `ARG OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=0` ka `docker build .` nkịtị (Railway na host ndị ọzọ a na-eji otu ọpịpị, yana ndị na-eme self-hosting n'onwe ha mgbe ụfọdụ) ghara ịbụ nke OOM gburu na builder nwere oke ebe nchekwa; nyefee `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=1` iji Turbopack wuo image ahụ. Lee `docs/guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md` → Akụrụngwa oge mwube. |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`             | _(edobeghị)_                               | `src/lib/db/core.ts` / `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`                       | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji mafe nyocha ahụike iguzosi ike n'ezi ihe SQLite mgbe sistemụ na-amalite. Ọ bara uru maka mmalite ngwa ngwa na database buru ibu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `NOTIFY_SOCKET`                             | _(edobeghị)_                               | systemd (protocol sd_notify)                                             | systemd na-edobe ya mgbe process na-arụ ọrụ n'okpuru service unit nwere njikọta sd_notify; OmniRoute na-agụ ya (lee `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`) iji zipụ ọkwa READY/WATCHDOG. Onye ọrụ ekwesịghị idebe ya ma ọlị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`               | _(edobeghị)_                               | `scripts/dev/systemd-notify.mjs`                                         | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji gbanyụọ systemd sd_notify (Type=notify / WatchdogSec=) ọbụlagodi mgbe ọ na-arụ ọrụ n'okpuru systemd unit.
