## 7. URL & Mmekọrịta Cloud

| Variable                                 | Ndabara                                         | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `BASE_URL`                               | `http://localhost:20128`                        | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL dị n'akụkụ sava nke ọrụ mmekọrịta dị n'ime na-eji akpọ `/api/sync/cloud`. Debe nke a ka ọ bụrụ URL loopback/container ọbụlagodi mgbe e ji proxy mee ka ngwa ahụ dị n'ihu ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLOUD_URL`                              | _(ọ tọgbọ chakoo)_                              | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL nke endpoint relay cloud (njirimara premium).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLOUD_SYNC_TIMEOUT_MS`                  | `12000`                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Oge HTTP ga-echere tupu ọ kwụsị arịrịọ mmekọrịta cloud.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_PROFILE`                | `full`                                          | Nhazi build Webpack                              | Profaịlụ oge-build (tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `minimal` iji wepụ modul ndị nwere ikike pụrụ iche n'ụzọ anụ ahụ na bundle).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`               | _mmepụta standalone nke .build/_                | `scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`          | Ntọgharị oge-build maka ndekọ mmepụta standalone nke usoro colocation mgbe build gachara na-eji. Nke a abụghị ntọala runtime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET`            | _(ọ tọgbọ chakoo)_                              | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Nzuzo e kekọrọ ọnụ nke a na-eji enyocha mbinye aka HMAC-SHA256 nke nzaghachi Cloud Sync.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRETS`           | `false`                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji kwe ka endpoint Cloud Sync dochie credentials mpaghara. Ndabara bụ `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_ENFORCE_SIGNATURE` | `false`                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji jụ nzaghachi Cloud Sync na-enweghị mbinye aka mgbe ahazighị nzuzo mpaghara (#13679). A na-enyocha mbinye aka dị adị mgbe niile — a na-ajụkwa ya mgbe niile ma ọ bụrụ na edoghị `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET` — n'agbanyeghị flag a. Na v3.9, ndabara ga-agbanwe ka mmanye dị.                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ZED_IMPORT_LEGACY_ONE_STEP`   | `false`                                         | `src/app/api/providers/zed/import/route.ts`      | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji laghachi na omume otu-nzọụkwụ v3.8.5 nke “bubata ihe niile” na-enweghị nkwenye onye ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                   | `http://localhost:20128`                        | OAuth, Dashboard, mmekọrịta                      | URL ihu-ọha maka OAuth redirect_uri, njikọ Dashboard, na URL ọhaneze emepụtara. Tọọ nke a ka ọ bụrụ URL ọhaneze kwụsiri ike mgbe OAuth callbacks ma ọ bụ njikọ ihe nchọgharị emepụtara ga-eji host reverse-proxy canonical.                                                                                                                                                          |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL`                  | _(ọ tọgbọ chakoo)_                              | Akụkụ client                                     | Oyiyi akụkụ client nke `CLOUD_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL`                    | _(ahazighị)_                                    | `src/shared/services/cloudSyncScheduler.ts`      | Nhọrọ ndabere ochie maka `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_PORT`                       | _(ahazighị — na-adaghachi na `PORT`)_           | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`          | Port ndabere nke akụkụ onye ahịa maka URL ngosi mgbe a maghị origin (SSR/nnwale); a na-agụ ya tupu `PORT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`              | _(ahazighị)_                                    | Ihe na-achọpụta origin ọha, URL onyonyo          | Origin OmniRoute nke ihe nchọgharị na-eji, nke nwere ihe kacha mkpa maka imepụta URL ọha na nkwado izi ezi nke origin ihe nchọgharị na-abụghị dashboard. Hazie nke a mgbe OpenWebUI ma ọ bụ relay ọzọ na-erute OmniRoute site na URL ime ụlọ mana ihe nchọgharị onye ọrụ ga-ebudata mgbasa ozi emepụtara site na LAN, tunnel, ma ọ bụ origin ọha. Etinyekwala `/v1`.                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL`        | _(ahazighị)_                                    | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | URL zuru ezu nke manifest plugin provider a na-akpọsa nye ndị ahịa sidecar. Mgbe ahazighị ya, OmniRoute na-ewepụta `/api/v1/provider-plugin-manifest` site na origin arịrịọ ma ọ bụ HOST/PORT.                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_PROTOCOL`              | `http`                                          | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | Protocol a na-eji mgbe a na-ewepụta URL manifest plugin provider site na HOST/PORT n'enweghị origin arịrịọ. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `https` n'azụ proxy ọha na-akwụsị TLS mgbe edoghị `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL` kpọmkwem.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY`                  | _(ahazighị)_                                    | `src/server/origin/publicOrigin.ts`              | Ụdị ntụkwasị obi nhọrọ maka header origin ọha ezigharịrị. Ahazighị = atụkwasịla `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` obi maka mkpebi nchekwa. `true` / `loopback` na-atụkwasị host/proto ezigharịrị obi naanị site na proxy loopback nwere akara token. `private` / `lan` na-atụkwasịkwa ndị proxy ibe dị na LAN nzuzo obi. Họrọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` akọwapụtara kpọmkwem na production. |
| `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`                       | _(ahazighị)_                                    | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | URL callback ọha maka ọrụ kie.ai asynchronous. Ntọala mgbanwe nwere ihe kacha mkpa tupu `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL` na `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL`             | _(ahazighị)_                                    | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Ụzọ ọzọ e si ede `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`. Ọ na-adaghachi na nke a mgbe ahazighị mgbanwe bụ isi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`                   | _(ahazighị)_                                    | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Origin ọha a na-eji emepụta URL callback asynchronous. Nhọrọ ndabere nwere ihe kacha nta mkpa maka callback kie.ai; a na-ejikwa ya dị ka URL ọha izugbe maka relay ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CROF_USAGE_URL`               | `https://crof.ai/usage_api/`                    | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Endpoint nyocha oke CrofAI nke ibe Usage na-eji. Dochie ya maka relay / fixture nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPENCODE_QUOTA_URL`           | `https://opencode.ai/zen/go/v1/usage`           | `open-sse/services/opencodeQuotaFetcher.ts`      | Endpoint ojiji OpenCode Go gọọmentị nke API key na-eme nkwenye njirimara, nke ibe Usage na-eji. Dochie ya maka relay / fixture nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS`        | `true`                                          | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Mepụta header njirimara OpenCode CLI (User-Agent, x-opencode-client/project, UUID arịrịọ/session) na arịrịọ opencode-go/zen upstream nke onye ahịa ezipụghị, ka Cloudflare na VPS egress wee nabata ha (#6210/#5997). A na-agbanye ya na ndabara kemgbe #10571; jiri `false`/`0`/`no`/`off` gbanyụọ ya.                                                                              |
| `OPENCODE_USER_AGENT`                    | `opencode/1.18.31`                              | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`              | User-Agent ndabara a na-eji mgbe agbanyere `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` ma edoghị ntọala dochie `<PROVIDER>_USER_AGENT` maka provider ọ bụla. A na-etinye ya naanị na executor opencode. N'arịrịọ na-enweghị key nke upstream na-egbochi, uru ahaziri nke na-enweghị `opencode/<version >= 1.17>` ga-eji ndabara a dochie ya kama ịjụ ya.                                       |
| `OPENCODE_CLIENT`                        | `desktop`                                       | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Uru maka nkụnye `x-opencode-client` emepụtara mgbe agbanyere `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OPENCODE_PROJECT`                       | `global`                                        | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Uru maka nkụnye `x-opencode-project` emepụtara mgbe agbanyere `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_REQUEST_CONTRACT`    | _(edoghị)_                                      | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `off` iji kwụsị imegharị body nke arịrịọ OpenCode na-enweghị igodo (akara streaming na ndepụta ngwá ọrụ). A ka na-etinye nkụnye ndị ahụ. A na-agụ ya n'arịrịọ ọ bụla, ya mere mgbanwe na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_PLACEHOLDER_TOOLS`   | _(edoghị)_                                      | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Aha ngwá ọrụ ndị e ji rikoma kewaa, nke a ga-ekwupụta n'arịrịọ OpenCode na-enweghị igodo nke na-ebughị nke ọ bụla, mgbe a hụbeghị ihe ọ bụla maka model ahụ. Ọ bụrụ na ọ tọgbọ chakoo, a na-eji otu ngwá ọrụ ndochi nke a gwara model ka ọ ghara ịkpọ. Ọ kachasị ndebanye 32, `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}`; a na-eleghara ndebanye ndị na-adabaghị anya.                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_URL`       | `https://ollama.com/settings`                   | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | URL ntọala Ollama Cloud a na-eji ewepụta ozi oke ojiji. Dochie ya maka relay / ihe nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`                    | _(edoghị)_                                      | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Kuki Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` a na-eji ewepụta ozi oke ojiji na peeji ntọala. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ, họrọ oghere Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_COOKIE`              | _(edoghị)_                                      | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Env var ọzọ maka kuki Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session`. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ, họrọ oghere Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`          | _(edoghị)_                                      | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Env var ọzọ maka kuki Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session`, nke a na-eji tupu aha mkpirikpi ndị ọzọ. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ, họrọ oghere Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`       | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com` | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Endpoint oke ojiji CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro). Dochie ya maka relay / ihe nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Mgbe ị na-ebunye ya n'azụ reverse proxy (nginx, Caddy), tọọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` ka ọ bụrụ URL ọha kwụsiri ike nke gị (dịka, `https://omniroute.example.com`) mgbe callback OAuth ma ọ bụ njikọ ọha emepụtara ga-eji hostname ahụ. Na-enweghị nke a, callback OAuth nwere ike ịda n'ihi na redirect_uri agaghị adaba, njikọ ọha emepụtara nwekwara ike ịtụ aka na origin dị n'ime container.
>
> Debe `BASE_URL` dịka URL loopback/container dị n'ime maka ọrụ server-to-server. Ejila `Origin` nke browser ma ọ bụ hostname ọha maka self-fetch dị n'ime nke na-ebu credential.
>
> Ndebanye dashboard e nyochara njirimara anaghị achọ URL ọha static: dashboard na-ezipụ arịrịọ same-origin na-adịghị nchebe yana token CSRF ejikọtara na session. OmniRoute ka na-eme ka nkwado public-origin dị n'otu ebe maka njikọ browser ndị na-abụghị dashboard: a na-ebu ụzọ tụkwasị env var URL ọha akọwapụtara obi; a na-eleghara nkụnye `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` raw anya belụsọ ma agbanyere `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` ma tinyekwa token na peer proxy dị nso iji gosi na a tụkwasịrị ya obi. Ejila ntọala CORS dozie arịrịọ dashboard same-origin; CORS bụ naanị maka client browser cross-origin.

---

## 8. Proxy Ọpụpụ

Ziga oku ndị na-eweta LLM dị n'elu site na proxy HTTP ma ọ bụ SOCKS5 maka njikwa ọpụpụ, nhazi ụzọ dabere na mpaghara, ma ọ bụ izochi IP.

| Variable                                 | Ndabara      | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                              | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ---------------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`       | `open-sse/executors`                           | Mee ka proxy agent SOCKS5 rụọ ọrụ maka oku ndị dị n'elu. Jiri `false` kwụsị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`       | Akụkụ client                                   | Ime ka akụkụ client mara na SOCKS5 dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `true`       | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Feature flag nke na-arụ ọrụ na ndabara (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala dochie anya dị na DB dashboard ga-emeri). Otu proxy na ntụgharị nke akaụntụ ọ bụla ga-akwụsị ịhọrọ ọzọ otu onye dara n'oge na-adịbeghị anya (nnwale TCP a jụrụ, ma ọ bụ 429 sitere na ya) ruo oge na-abawanye okpukpu abụọ mgbe ọ bụla ọdịda ahụ mere ọzọ, ruo n'ókè kachasị. `false` na-eweghachi nhọrọ nkịtị.                                                                         |
| `SELECTOR_CONTROL_ALLOWLIST`             | _(edobeghị)_ | `src/lib/proxySubscription/selectorGuard.ts`   | Ndepụta ndị anabatara maka ebe selector-control nke local-core karịrị loopback (hosts ma ọ bụ IP ndị e ji rikoma/oghere kewaa; `*` na-emepe LAN/private mana ọ naghị emeghe metadata/link-local). A na-anabata loopback site na http/https na ndabara. Uru dị na DB dashboard ga-emeri variable a mgbe edobere ya ma ọ bụghị oghere.                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_BASE_MS`                | `120000`     | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Oge ntọala (ms) nke iwepụ 429 nwa oge, nke na-abawanye okpukpu abụọ mgbe ọ bụla o megharịrị. A na-agụ ya otu ugboro mgbe usoro na-amalite. A kpachiri ya na `1000..3600000`; uru karịrị oke ma ọ bụ nke a na-apụghị ịgụ ga-alaghachi na ndabara ma nye ịdọ aka ná ntị.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_MAX_MS`                 | `3600000`    | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Oke kachasị (ms) nke iwepụ 429 nwa oge. A na-agụ ya otu ugboro mgbe usoro na-amalite. A kpachiri ya na `1000..3600000`; uru karịrị oke ma ọ bụ nke a na-apụghị ịgụ ga-alaghachi na ndabara ma nye ịdọ aka ná ntị. Mgbe oke kachasị dị n'okpuru ntọala, ọ ga-alaghachi na ndabara. Ibelata oke kachasị na-ebelatakwa oge ichefu usoro ọdịda: a na-ehichapụ ndenye `2 x max` mgbe oge ha gwụsịrị.                                                                                    |
| `PROXY_POOL_SHARED_EGRESS_ORDER`         | `false`      | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Feature flag a na-ahọrọ ịgbanye (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ntọala dochie anya dị na DB dashboard ga-emeri). Maka ndị na-eweta ọrụ nke a na-ekewa oke ojiji ha dịka adreesị ọpụpụ si dị, dobe onye so n'otu pool nke na-eji otu adreesị ọpụpụ ahụrụ anya dịka onye a jụrụ n'oge na-adịbeghị anya ozugbo n'okpuru ndị ahụike. Ọ na-emetụta naanị usoro, anaghị ewepụ onye ọ bụla. Ọ chọrọ `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`, nke na-emepụta akara ngosi ọjụjụ ọ na-agụ. |
| `PROXY_WEBHOOK_REBOUND_MS`               | `300000`     | `src/lib/proxyEvents/proxyTransitionBridge.ts` | Ohere rebound (ms) nke na-amachi ọkwa webhook `proxy.set_aside` ugboro ugboro maka onye ọ bụla nọ na pool ka ọtụtụ ọjụjụ n'otu oge ziga naanị otu event. A na-agụ ya mgbe ọ bụla a na-ezipụ event. A kpachiri ya na `1000..3600000`; uru karịrị oke ma ọ bụ nke a na-apụghị ịgụ ga-alaghachi na ndabara na-agbachi nkịtị.                                                                                                                                                          |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(edobeghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Proxy HTTP maka oku ndị dị n'elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(edobeghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Proxy HTTPS maka oku ndị dị n'elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(edobeghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Proxy zuru ụwa ọnụ (na-akwado `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(edoghị)_   | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                   | Na-ekekọta ebe echo-IP nyocha ọpụpụ proxy na-eji n'otu URL. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, nyocha ahụ na-anwale `api64.ipify.org` tupu `api4.ipify.org` ka a ghara ịkọ na tunnel ndị na-akwado naanị IPv4 anwụọla (#9694).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(edoghị)_   | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Aha nnabata/IP ndị e ji rikoma kewaa nke a ga-agafe proxy maka ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`         | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`            | Ọnụ ọgụgụ socket kacha elu nwere ike ịrụ ọrụ n'otu oge maka dispatcher proxy HTTP/SOCKS ọ bụla echekwara na cache. Stream SSE ndị na-adịte aka dịka Codex `/v1/responses` chọrọ njikọ karịrị otu mgbe ọtụtụ arịrịọ na-eji otu proxy nke ọkwa akaụntụ. A na-amachi ụkpụrụ karịrị `256`.                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`      | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts`   | Oge nkwụsị handshake (connect) SOCKS5 na ms. Bulie ya mgbe nnukwu arịrịọ n'otu oge rutere otu host gateway obibi (dịka ọmụmaatụ, arịrịọ 100 n'otu oge) — handshake n'ezie nwere ike ịgafe 10s mgbe pool jupụtara n'agbanyeghị na proxy ka dị irè, nke ma ọ bụghị ya ga-apụta dị ka ozi ụgha `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable`. A na-amachi ya na `120000`.                                                                                                                     |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`      | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`              | Mgbe ọ bụ `false` (ndabara), arịrịọ nke proxy e kenyere ya na-enweghị ike ịchọta adreesị ya ka a na-**ajụ (fail-closed)** kama ịlaghachi n'iji njikọ ozugbo — nke a na-egbochi mkpughe real-IP. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji weghachite ndaghachi DIRECT ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`      | `open-sse/executors`                           | Jiri wreq-js mee ka fingerprint TLS yie nke ọzọ (na-eṅomi Chrome 124). Nke a na-egbochi mgbochi JA3/JA4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(edoghị)_   | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                 | Ndepụta provider e ji rikoma kewaa nke a na-anabata maka ụzọ TLS ọhụrụ a na-esi na proxy agafe (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). TLS ozugbo na-edowe omume ochie ya mgbe edoghị ya; naanị provider ndị a ka a na-ebuga site na bridge fingerprint Chrome-124.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`      | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts`   | Kwe ka context browser Claude Turnstile Playwright leghara njehie asambodo HTTPS anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

### Ọnọdụ eji ya eme ihe

| Ọnọdụ                                         | Nhazi                                                                                                                       |
| --------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SOCKS5 site na tunnel SSH**                 | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                             |
| **Proxy HTTP ụlọ ọrụ**                        | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com`   |
| **Mgbochi fingerprint**                       | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` — chọrọ `wreq-js` (etinyere ya)                                                               |
| **Ọpụpụ a na-achịkwa / enweghị ohere ozugbo** | Hapụ `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (ndabara). Arịrịọ ga-ada kpamkpam mgbe proxy adịghị kama ịpụpụ site na njikọ ozugbo.           |
| **Ochie / mmepe — kwe ka ndaghachi ozugbo**   | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Na-eweghachite omume tupu e mee ka nchekwa sie ike: a na-eji njikọ ozugbo mgbe nchọpụta proxy dara. |

> **Mara (ịgafe nyocha NVIDIA — #3226):** Ebe njedebe NVIDIA maka nyocha API-key
> na-akwụsị mgbe e zigara ya site na fetch zuru ụwa ọnụ nke proxy/TLS gbanwere (undici dispatcher → 504).
> `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` na-ama ụma agafe
> mgbanwe proxy maka naanị oku nyocha ahụ site n'iji `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`.
> Nke a bụ ewepụga e dekọrọ ma kpachie ókè ya — ọ **metụtaghị** ọpụpụ chat/usage.
> `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts` na-eme ka ókè nke ngafe ahụ ghara ịgbanwe.

---

## 9. Njikọ Ngwaọrụ CLI

Na-achịkwa etu OmniRoute si achọpụta ma malite sidecar CLI (Claude Code, Codex, wdg.).

| Mgbanwe                           | Ndabara                          | Faịlụ Isi Mbido                                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | `auto` = chọọ na PATH sistemụ; `manual` = jiri naanị ụzọ ndị akọwapụtara kpọmkwem.                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(edobeghị)_                     | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ndenye PATH ndị ọzọ maka ịchọta faịlụ binary CLI (e ji kọlọn kewaa ha).                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(edobeghị)_                     | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Dochie ndekọ ụlọ eji agụ nhazi CLI (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Ọ ga-abụrịrị ụzọ zuru ezu ma dịkwa n'ime ụlọ usoro ahụ — **ma ọ bụ**, n'ime container, ụzọ e ji bind-mount tinye (ọ bụ otu a ka `/host-home` si arụ ọrụ). Ihe ọ bụla ọzọ ga-alaghachi na ndekọ ụlọ. |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Kwe ka OmniRoute dee faịlụ nhazi CLI (mmelite token, data session). Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji mee ka ide ọ bụla na nhazi CLI daa, tinyere njehie doro anya nke sịrị "agbanyụrụ ide ihe".                                                                       |
| `CLI_CLAUDE_BIN`                  | `claude`                         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Claude CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CLI_CODEX_BIN`                   | `codex`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Codex CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_DROID_BIN`                   | `droid`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Droid CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_OPENCLAW_BIN`                | `openclaw`                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary OpenClaw CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CLI_CURSOR_BIN`                  | `agent`, emesịa `cursor`         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary agent nke Cursor. Ọ bụrụ na ọ dịghị, nchọpụta ga-ebu ụzọ nwaa `agent`, ma ọ daa, jiri `cursor`.                                                                                                                              |
| `CLI_CLINE_BIN`                   | `cline`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Cline CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_5DIVE_BIN`                   | `5dive`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary 5dive CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_5DIVE_STATE_DIR`             | `/var/lib/5dive`                 | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ndekọ ọnọdụ sistemụ nke 5dive (profaịlụ nkwenye nke root nwe); ọ na-eme ka ndabara STATE_DIR nke 5dive kwekọọ.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLI_CONTINUE_BIN`                | `cn`                             | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Continue CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CLI_QODER_BIN`                   | `qodercli`                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_QWEN_BIN`                    | `qwen`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Qwen Code CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CLI_AIDER_BIN`                   | `aider`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri iche gaa na faịlụ binary Aider CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

Ọ bụrụ na nke a adịghị, ọ ga-eji `CLI_AUGGIE_BIN`, ma mechaa chọọ na PATH.                                                                       |
