| `ZCODE_SERVER_ENTRY`              | `<runtimeRoot>/zcode-server.cjs` | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Skripụ ntinye sava-ngwa nke a na-eji kwado sava ZCode.                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ZCODE_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS`        | `10000`                          | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Oge nchere mmalite (ms) tupu e were na mbido sava-ngwa ZCode adaala.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ZCODE_RPC_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `30000`                          | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Oge nchere RPC maka arịrịọ ọ bụla (ms) maka oku sava-ngwa ZCode.                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ZCODE_TURN_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `120000`                         | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Ogologo oge kachasị (ms) maka otu ntụgharị ZCode tupu onye nlekọta akwụsị ya n'ihi oge agwụla.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ZCODE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`          | `250`                            | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Nkeji oge nyocha ugboro ugboro (ms) maka mmecha ntụgharị ZCode.                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `HERMES_HOME`                     | `~/.hermes`                      | `src/lib/cli-helper/config-generator/hermesHome.ts` | Ndekọ ebe obibi Hermes Agent ebe OmniRoute na-agụ/na-ede nhazi Hermes CLI. Ọ dabara na env var nke ihe nrụnye Hermes PowerShell na-edobe na Windows (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\hermes`).                                                                                    |

### Mmekọrịta Akpaghị Aka nke Profaịlụ CLI

A naghị agbanye ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ ndị a na ndabara; a ga-eji aka họrọ ịgbanye ha. A pụkwara ịgbanye ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ ha site na
dashboard CLI Code.

| Mgbanwe                               | Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi                                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Mgbe mmekọrịta ụdị onye na-enye ọrụ gasịrị, ọ na-eji katalọgụ dị ugbu a degharịa faịlụ profaịlụ `~/.codex/*.config.toml` na-akpaghị aka. Ọ chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-agbanwe nhazi Codex nọ n'ọrụ/nke ndabara, nkwenye njirimara, ntọala Codex-lb, ma ọ bụ nhọrọ onye na-enye ọrụ.              |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Mgbe mmekọrịta ụdị onye na-enye ọrụ gasịrị, ọ na-eji katalọgụ dị ugbu a degharịa faịlụ profaịlụ Claude Code `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` na-akpaghị aka. Ọ chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-agbanwe nhazi Claude nọ n'ọrụ/nke ndabara, nkwenye njirimara, ma ọ bụ nhọrọ onye na-enye ọrụ. |

### Ọmụmaatụ Docker

```bash
# Tinye binary ndị dị na kọmputa nnabata n'ime konteena ma gwa OmniRoute ebe ha nọ:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ naanị mgbe e mere ka ụzọ ahụ bụrụ bind mount sitere na
host — jikọta ya na mount dịka `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (lee profaịlụ
`host` dị na `docker-compose.yml`). A na-eleghara ụzọ na-adịghị n'ime home
onye ọrụ container ma ọ bụ na-abụghị bind mount anya, n'ihi na a ga-atụfu ihe
e dere ebe ahụ mgbe e megharịrị container ahụ.

Image ahụ na-arụ ọrụ dịka `USER node`, ya mere `/root` na-enweghị mount abụghị
override ziri ezi.

| Mgbanwe                                  | Ndabara         | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                    |
| ---------------------------------------- | --------------- | ----------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(akpaghị aka)_ | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Manye nchọpụta container ka ọ dị ọkụ (`1`/`true`) ma ọ bụ ka ọ gbanyụọ (`0`/`false`). A chọrọ nke a naanị na runtime ndị nchọpụta akpaghị aka na-ahụghị. |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Kwe ka CLI-tool dee nhazi n'ụzọ container na-enweghị mount n'agbanyeghị nke ahụ. Ihe dakọtara na CLI bụ `--allow-container-write`.                       |

### Ngwa enyemaka Binary CLI (`omniroute`)

Mgbanwe ndị a na-ahazi omume nke binary CLI `omniroute` n'onwe ya (ọ bụghị
nchọpụta sidecar dị n'elu).

| Mgbanwe                      | Ndabara        | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                       | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------- | -------------- | --------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`             | _(sistemu)_    | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Manye asụsụ mmepụta CLI. Locale BCP-47 (dịka `en`, `pt-BR`). Ọ na-akagbu env vars locale sistemu (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`         | _(edobebeghị)_ | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji zipụ stdout/stderr nke server gaa na terminal n'ụdị nlekọta. Ọ dakọtara na flag `--log` na `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`        | _(edobebeghị)_ | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Token machine-auth etinyere dịka header `x-omniroute-cli-token`. A na-emepụta ya na-akpaghị aka na task 8.12.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `30000`        | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Ogologo oge HTTP maka nnwale ọ bụla (ms) maka arịrịọ CLI → server.                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS` | `60000`        | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Oge kachasị (ms) CLI ga-echere endpoint ahụike server tupu o bipụta ịdọ aka ná ntị timeout. Ọ bara uru maka cold start ndị na-egbu oge (dịka Windows). A pụkwara ịtọ ya site na `--ready-timeout`.                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`          | `0`            | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji bipụta nchọpụta retry/backoff na stderr mgbe iwu CLI na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`      | _(edobebeghị)_ | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Directory ahaziri iche maka nchọpụta plugin CLI (ngwugwu `omniroute-cmd-*`). Mgbe edobebeghị ya, ndabara ya bụ `~/.omniroute/plugins/`. Ọ bụ naanị maka CLI — ọ naghị erute plugin scanner nke server, nke `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` na-atụ aka na ya (ngalaba 2). |

---

## 10. Njikọ Agent Dị N'ime & MCP

| Mgbanwe                                         | Ndabara                                             | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                                               | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`                            | nchọpụta-akpaka                                     | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | URL akọwapụtara nke ngwaọrụ MCP/A2A ga-eji ruo OmniRoute. Ọ na-akagbu nchọpụta-akpaka localhost.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(edoghị)_                                          | Modul MCP/A2A                                               | Igodo API maka oku ngwaọrụ MCP dị n'ime na oku nkà A2A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(edoghị)_                                          | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | ID igodo maka ikenye ndekọ nyocha MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(edoghị)_                                          | Nke ochie                                                   | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Ụbọchị a ga-edobe akụkọ ihe aga-eme A2A na ọdụ data mpaghara tupu nhichapụ kwa ụbọchị ehichapụ otu ahịrị. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, ọ bụghị ọnụọgụ, ma ọ bụ `<= 0`, ọ ga-alaghachi na `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Mgbanwe nkwụsị mberede maka atụmatụ nleba anya memory-hits nke A2A. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji mafere kpamkpam nyocha ncheta maka otu ọrụ; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (gụnyere mgbe edoghị ya) na-eme ka ọ nọgide na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Na-eme ka endpoint recorded-triage nke Issue Agent na-arụ na-anọghị n'ịntanetị/mpaghara nwee ike ịrụ ọrụ. Hapụ ya ka ọ bụrụ nke anaghị arụ ọrụ belụsọ mgbe a na-agba workflow recorded-triage mpaghara n'ụzọ doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(edoghị)_                                          | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Oge nkwụsị (ms) maka otu ọsọ recorded-triage nke Issue Agent. A na-amachibido ya na oke kachasị dị n'ime; ọ na-alaghachi na ndabara arụnyere mgbe edoghị ya ma ọ bụ mgbe ọ na-ezighi ezi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(context na-arụ ọrụ)_                              | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | Context/profaịlụ CLI nke remote-mode maka iwu `omniroute`; ọ na-akagbu context na-arụ ọrụ dị na nchekwa context mpaghara. Ya na `--context <name>` bụ otu ihe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Gbanyụọ backend keychain OS `keytar` nhọrọ maka nzere context CLI. Mgbe agbanyere ya, nzere ga-anọgide na ọnọdụ `0600` nke `config.json`, CLI ga-ewepụkwa ịdọ aka ná ntị fallback otu ugboro; e zubere ya maka ọrụ headless/container a kpachapụrụ anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Manye njikwa nnweta dabere na scope n'oku ngwaọrụ MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SCOPES`                          | _(niile)_                                           | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Scope ndị e ji rikoma kewaa: `admin`, `combos`, `health`, `models`, `routing`, `budget`, `metrics`, `pricing`, `memory`, `skills`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_COMPRESS_DESCRIPTIONS`           | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Kpakọọ nkọwa ngwaọrụ MCP tupu ime serialization nke manifest. Uru ndị na-eme ka ọ rụọ ọrụ: `1`, `true`, `on`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_DESCRIPTION_COMPRESSION`         | `rtk`                                               | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Algọridim/profaịlụ mkpakọ. Uru ndị na-agbanyụ ya: `0`, `false`, `off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                | `10000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Oke oge nkwụsị (ms) maka ọgụgụ njikwa dị n'ime MCP-server (health, resilience, combos, quota, usage).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Oke oge nkwụsị (ms) maka ngafe MCP ndị na-echere onye na-eweta ọrụ (`route_request`, `web_search`, `web_fetch`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                   | `5`                                                 | `src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`                         | Ọnụọgụ kachasị elu nke ọnọdụ ndeksi corpus mpaghara echekwara na ebe nchekwa (LRU, otu maka ndekọ mgbọrọgwụ ọ bụla e tinyere na ndeksi). A na-amachi ya ka ọ ghara ịdị n'okpuru `1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MODEL_SYNC_INTERVAL_HOURS`                     | `24`                                                | `src/shared/services/modelSyncScheduler.ts`                 | Oge etiti mmekọrịta katalọgụ model n'ime awa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oge etiti nlele oke ọnụego na oke ojiji nke onye na-eweta ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oghere oge (ms) n'etiti nnweta oke OAuth ndị na-esochi ibe ha n'ime nnukwu mmekọrịta; a na-enweta njikọ OAuth otu otu iji zere iziga ọtụtụ arịrịọ n'otu ntabi anya na sistemụ dị n'elu. `0` na-ewepụ nke a (n'otu oge).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Oge kacha nta (ms) n'etiti nnweta oke ndị na-esochi ibe ha site na sistemụ dị n'elu n'ụzọ nyocha mbụ/nlekota nke arịrịọ ọ bụla; ọ na-ekewa oku netwọkụ ndị na-eme n'otu oge ka ọtụtụ akaụntụ dị n'otu IP ghara iziga ọtụtụ arịrịọ n'otu ntabi anya na sistemụ dị n'elu. E jikọtara ya na ndị na-eweta nnweta oke Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (ebe nnweta abụọ ahụ), OpenCode, na Crof (#6009, #6911). Ụzọ izugbe `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` nke na-ekesa arịrịọ (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/etc.) akwadobeghị — a na-enyocha ya iche. Nke a anaghị emetụta nsonaazụ sitere na cache. `0` na-agbanyụ ya; a na-amachi ya n'etiti `0..5000`. |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_POST_USAGE_REFRESH_DELAY_MS`   | `5000`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oge nchere (ms) tupu emelite oke onye na-eweta ọrụ mgbe ihe omume ojiji n'ezie gasịrị, iji nye API oke nke sistemụ dị n'elu oge ịdebanye ojiji ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | nchọpụta-akpaghị-aka                                | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`             | Ụzọ zuru oke gaa na faịlụ mmemme Chrome ma ọ bụ Edge nke sistemụ, nke a na-eji maka nbanye Adobe Firefly mmekọrịta na mmelite na-anọghị n'ihuenyo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`                 | agbanyere                                           | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Na-eme ka ọnọdụ IMS na ihe ize ndụ ihe nchọgharị dị ọhụrụ site na nnọkọ Chrome CDP akọwapụtara maka akaụntụ. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ mmelite ihe nchọgharị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`                    | agbanyere                                           | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Na-echekwa nnọkọ Adobe ndị a rụziri n'okpuru `DATA_DIR` n'agbanyeghị mmalitegharị usoro. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji debe nnọkọ naanị na ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`               | `12000`                                             | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Oghere oge kacha nta n'ime milisekọnd n'etiti nnyefe mmepụta Adobe Firefly; `0` na-agbanyụ nkewa oge ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`              | `15000`                                             | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Oge ezumike mgbakwunye n'ime milisekọnd mgbe nnyefe Adobe nke atọ ọ bụla gara nke ọma.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS`            | `8000`                                              | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`                   | Oge ndabere ichere n'ime milisekọnd mgbe nzaghachi Adobe 408 nwa oge gasịrị; a na-ejikọta ya na nkewa oge nnyefe n'ime nnwale na-agaghị agafe ise.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES`         | `false`                                             | `src/instrumentation-node.ts`                               | Gbanyụọ ọrụ ndabere niile (mmekọrịta, ọnụahịa, mmelite model). Ọ bara uru maka CI/nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_RUNTIME_BACKGROUND_TASKS`     | _(edoghị ya)_                                       | `src/lib/config/runtimeSettings.ts`                         | Manye ọrụ ndabere ka ha rụọ ọrụ mgbe achọpụtara nnwale akpaghị aka. Tọọ `1` iji kagbuo usoro nghọta nnwale ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUDGET_RESET_JOB_INTERVAL_MS`        | `600000`                                            | `src/lib/jobs/budgetResetJob.ts`                            | Ugboro nyocha nrụgharị mmefu ego (ms). Oke kacha nta bụ `10000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONNECTION_RECOVERY_INTERVAL_MS`     | `60000`                                             | `src/lib/quota/connectionRecovery.ts`                       | Ugboro mgbake njikọ mgbe oge nchere gafere (ms): ọ na-enyocha ọzọ njikọ ndị `rate_limited_until` nwa oge ha gafere, na-abụghị n'ụzọ arịrịọ a na-ejikarị. Oke kacha nta `5000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONNECTION_RECOVERY`         | `false`                                             | `src/lib/quota/connectionRecovery.ts`                       | Gbanyụọ onye nhazi mgbake njikọ mgbe oge nchere gafere (`getProviderCredentials` ka na-eme mgbake umengwụ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_REASONING_CACHE_CLEANUP_INTERVAL_MS` | `1800000`                                           | `src/lib/jobs/reasoningCacheCleanupJob.ts`                  | Ugboro nhicha cache ntụgharị uche (ms). Oke kacha nta `60000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_REASONING_MIN_BUDGET`                | _edobeghị_ (agbanyụrụ)                              | `open-sse/services/reasoningTokenBuffer.ts`                 | Oke kacha nta a na-ahọrọ iji maka mmefu mmepụta ụdị echiche: a na-ebuli `max_tokens` nke onye na-akpọ oku dị na `[256, floor)` ruo n'oke kacha nta ahụ (ma oke mmepụta ụdị ga-amachi ya). Edobeghị = anaghị abawanye mmefu ndị ahịa ma ọlị (#9507).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOG_EXPORT_CRON`                     | `0 * * * *`                                         | `src/lib/jobs/logExportJob.ts`                              | Nkọwa Cron (UTC) maka ọrụ mbupụ ndekọ oku nke na-ekpochapụ ebe mbupụ ndekọ ọ bụla agbanyere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONFIG_HOT_RELOAD_MS`                | `5000`                                              | `src/lib/config/hotReload.ts`                               | Etiti oge nyocha (ms) maka ibugharị nhazi ozugbo. A na-ajụ uru dị ala karịa `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_REDIS_AUTH_CACHE`            | _(agbanyere)_                                       | `src/lib/db/apiKeys.ts`                                     | Tọọ `1` iji gafere cache nkwenye API-key nke Redis na-akwado (na-amanye ọgụgụ DB).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS`           | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/rtk/filterLoader.ts` | Tụkwasị iwu nzacha ọrụ RTK ndị onye ọrụ na-achịkwa obi na-enweghị nyocha mbinye aka siri ike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_LITE_MAX_TOOL_LENGTH`                | `2000`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/lite.ts`                     | Oke mkpụrụedemede maka mbelata nsonaazụ-ngwaọrụ Lite nke a na-eme tupu oge eruo mgbe edobeghị `lite.maxToolLength`. Nso 256–1000000. Ntọala dashboard na-emeri env a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKERS`                      | `2`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Ọnụọgụ kacha elu nke ndị ọrụ RTK/Caveman synchronous nwere ike ịrụ n'otu oge; ọrụ ndị gafere ya na-echere n'usoro FIFO.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKER_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `120000`                                            | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Oge ngwụcha maka ọrụ ọ bụla na milisekọnd. A na-akwụsị ndị ọrụ oge ha gwụrụ, arịrịọ ahụ adaakwa n'ụzọ mepere emepe n'enweghị mgbanwe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKER_IDLE_MS`               | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Ogologo oge ịnọ nkịtị na milisekọnd tupu a kwụsị onye ọrụ mkpakọ a na-ejighị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_ENABLED`          | `false`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Mgbanwe ukwu nke circuit-breaker maka injin ọ bụla n'ime stacked-pipeline T02. **Nhọrọ iji (agbanyụrụ na ndabara)** — mgbe agbanyere ya, a na-amafere injin na-atụpụ njehie ugboro ugboro n'ofe arịrịọ (fail-open) ruo oge nchere; gbanyụọ = omume ochie yiri onwe ya n'otu byte.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_THRESHOLD`        | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Ọnụọgụ ọdịda n'usoro n'ofe arịrịọ tupu breaker nke injin emeghee.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_COOLDOWN_MS`      | `30000`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Milisekọnd ole injin emeghe ga-anọ ka a na-amafere ya tupu nyocha half-open.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_RETRIEVAL_RAMP_FACTOR`         | `2`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | Mgbago nzaghachi iweghachite CCR T08/H8: iweghachite ọ bụla gara aga nke blọk echekwara na-ebuli `minChars` ya dị irè n'usoro kwụ ọtọ (ọdịnaya a na-eweghachite ugboro ugboro na-enweta mkpakọ pere mpe; iweghachite `>=3` = anaghị etinye mkpakọ ma ọlị). `1` na-agbanyụọ mgbago ahụ (naanị ịmafe binary n'oke ahụ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_DURABLE_STORE`                 | `true`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | Ebe nchekwa blọk CCR na-adịgide adịgide (#9061). Ọ na-eji SQLite akwado ebe nchekwa dị na memori ka blọk wee lanarị nchụpụ LRU, TTL, mmalite ọzọ, ma ọ bụ mgbe arịrịọ iweghachite rutere na instansị ọzọ. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji debe blọk naanị na memori. Blọk karịrị 512KB na runtime igwe ojii ga-anọgide naanị na memori n'agbanyeghị nke a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_ENABLED`             | `false`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | Mgbanwe ukwu maka ifriizi prefix T08/H5 nke ojiji chọpụtara. **A ga-ahọrọ itinye ya n'ọrụ (agbanyụrụ na ndabara)** — mgbe agbanyere ya, a na-ewere system prompt a hụrụ ugboro `>=` oke ahụ dị ka prefix kwụsiri ike nke enwere ike idobe na cache, ma chekwaa ya ka mkpakọ ghara imetụta ya ọbụna maka ndị na-eweta ọrụ nke heuristic cache static na-agaghị achọpụta (ifriizi naanị _na-echekwa_, ọ naghị agbanwe ihe).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_THRESHOLD`           | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | Ọnụọgụ ugboro a ga-ahụ system prompt tupu ewere ya dị ka prefix kwụsiri ike e friziri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_BOOTSTRAPPED`                        | `false`                                             | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx`                    | Script bootstrap na-edobe ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` mgbe nhazi mbụ gasịrị. Ọ na-achịkwa ma a ga-egosi ọkachamara nhazi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_BODY_PROJECT_OVERRIDE`         | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/executors/antigravity.ts`                         | Ụzọ mgbapụ: kwe ka body arịrịọ dochie field project Antigravity.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_CREDITS`                           | `off`                                               | `open-sse/services/antigravityCredits.ts`                   | Iwu kredit Google One AI: `off` anaghị etinye kredit ma ọlị, `retry` na-etinye ya otu ugboro mgbe quota 429 tozuru oke mere, ebe `always` na-etinye ya na arịrịọ mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_ALLOW_SIGNATURE_BYPASS`            | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-to-gemini.ts`           | Kwe ka onye ntụgharị arịrịọ Antigravity mafere nkwado siri ike nke mbinye aka arịrịọ CLI ya mgbe upstream jụrụ ezigbo mbinye aka (ọnọdụ debug/CLI-ochie). Uru na-abụghị efu na-eme ka mfefe ahụ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `AGY_TOKEN_FILE`                                | `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/antigravity-oauth-token` | `src/app/api/providers/agy-auth/apply-local/route.ts`       | Dochie ụzọ faịlụ token Antigravity CLI (agy) maka mbubata nbanye mpaghara nke nchọpụta akpaka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

### Njikọ OAuth CLI (N'ime)

| Ngbanwe             | Ndabara         | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite               | Nkọwa                                        |
| ------------------- | --------------- | ------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | nchọpụta akpaka | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | URL sava maka njikọ njirimara CLI↔OmniRoute. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(edobeghị)_    | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Token njirimara maka njikọ CLI.              |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`           | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | ID onye ọrụ maka seshọn njikọ CLI.           |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(edobeghị)_    | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.    |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(edobeghị)_    | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.     |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(edobeghị)_    | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.   |

## 11. OAuth Provider Credentials

Asambodo arụnyere n'ime maka **mmepe localhost**. Maka ntinye dịpụrụ adịpụ, debanye aha nke gị na njikwa onye nrụpụta nke onye na-enye ọrụ ọ bụla.

| Variable                                    | Provider                | Notes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| :------------------------------------------ | :---------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLAUDE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Claude Code (Anthropic) | Onye ahịa ọha — ọ dịghị ihe nzuzo achọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REDIRECT_URI`                  | Claude Code             | Gbanwee URI ntụgharị. Nke ndabara: `https://platform.claude.com/oauth/code/callback`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CODEX_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Codex / OpenAI          | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Gemini (Google)         | Chọrọ `_SECRET` kwekọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | Gemini (Google)         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `KIMI_CODING_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Kimi Coding (Moonshot)  | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MUSE_CODE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                 | Muse Code (Meta)        | Nhọrọ ịgbanwe ID onye ahịa Muse CLI device-flow ọha. Hapụ ka ọ ghara ịtọlite iji jiri onye ahịa ọha arụnyere n'ime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Antigravity (Google)    | Chọrọ `_SECRET` kwekọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`           | Antigravity (Google)    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot          | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GHE_COPILOT_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | GHE Copilot             | Nhọrọ ịgbanwe ID onye ahịa OAuth nke GitHub Enterprise Copilot. Ọ na-alaghachi na ndabara ọha nke `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` mgbe etinyeghị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `COPILOT_INTEGRATION_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot          | Nhọrọ ịgbanwe ID ntinye onye ahịa GitHub Copilot ezigara na `Copilot-Integration-Id` na `Editor-Plugin-Version` headers. Nke ndabara bụ `copilot-developer-cli`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `WINDSURF_API_KEY`                          | Windsurf / Devin (v3.8) | Igodo API ndabara ejiri site na `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` mgbe enweghị asambodo njikọ ọ bụla dị. Nhọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                             | Devin CLI (v3.8)        | Ụzọ omenala gaa na Devin CLI binary (`devin`). `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` na-edozi ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | ID onye ahịa OAuth maka GitLab Duo. Debanye aha ngwa na `https://gitlab.com/-/profile/applications` na URI ntụgharị `<NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/callback` na scopes `api, read_user, openid, profile, email`. Ọ na-alaghachi na `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`            | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Ihe nzuzo onye ahịa OAuth maka GitLab Duo. Nhọrọ — usoro PKCE anaghị achọ ihe nzuzo. Ọ na-alaghachi na `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`                       | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Gbanwee URL isi GitLab (GitLab nke onwe). Nke ndabara bụ `https://gitlab.com`. Ọ na-alaghachi na `GITLAB_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GITLAB_BASE_URL`                           | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Nlaghachi ochie maka `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`. A na-eji ya mgbe ahazighị ụdị `_DUO_`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Nlaghachi ochie maka `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` nke `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` na-eri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Nlaghachi ochie maka `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` nke `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` na-eri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                 | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `QODER_OAUTH_AUTHORIZE_URL`                 | Qoder                   | Hazie ka ịgbanwuo Qoder OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `QODER_OAUTH_TOKEN_URL`                     | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `QODER_OAUTH_USERINFO_URL`                  | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `QODER_PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN`               | Qoder                   | Nlaghachi igodo API ozugbo (na-agbagha OAuth).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`                       | Qoder                   | ID oghere ọrụ maka Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_QODER_WORKSPACE`                 | Qoder                   | Aha ọzọ maka `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `QODER_CLI_CONFIG_DIR`                      | Qoder                   | Gbanwee ndekọ nhazi Qoder CLI (nnọkọ PAT dịpụrụ adịpụ, na-ezere imebi nbanye ihe nchọgharị).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `BLACKBOX_WEB_VALIDATED_TOKEN`              | Blackbox Web            | Akara `tk` n'ihu ihu iji zipụ dị ka `validated` na `/api/chat`. Achọrọ mgbe Blackbox na-amanye ijikọ akara; ma ọ bụghị ya, OmniRoute na-alaghachi na UUID na-enweghị usoro. Lee nsogbu #2252.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_BASE_URL`                    | Vision Bridge guardrail | URL isi dakọtara na OpenAI maka oku nkwụnye ụzọ ọhụụ na-abụghị Anthropic. Na-agbanwe na gburugburu URL OpenAI ochie ma ọ bụ api.openai.com. Tụnye na `/v1` nke OmniRoute ma ọ bụ ebe njedebe ọ bụla dakọtara na OpenAI (Gemini OpenAI-compat, OpenRouter). Nsogbu #2232. Mgbe URL bụ `/v1` nke OmniRoute, arịrịọ sub-nkọwa na-eziga `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` ma na-enyocha ya na asambodo njikọ onwe ya edoziri (`sk_omniroute` sentinel na ọnọdụ mpaghara, ma ọ bụ `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) ka ntinye `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` wee rụọ ọrụ. |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`                     | Vision Bridge guardrail | Igodo API maka URL dị n'elu. Na-agbanwe gburugburu OpenAI / Google gburugburu maka oku nkwụnye ụzọ ọhụụ na-abụghị Anthropic. Ụdị Anthropic na-edebe ụzọ igodo Anthropic raara onwe ha nye. Nsogbu #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VISION_BRIDGE_NEGATIVE_CACHE_MS` | Vision Bridge guardrail | Ogologo oge a na-echekwa nsonaazụ ụzọ "enweghị onye ruru eru" na cache, na ms (ndabara `30000`). Uru na-ezighi ezi ma ọ bụ na-adịghị mma na-alaghachi na ndabara; `0` na-agbanyụ cache na-adịghị mma. Isi mmalite: `src/lib/guardrails/visionBridgeRouter.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Gaa na [Google Cloud Console → Credentials](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials)
> 2. Mepụta ID Onye Ahịa OAuth 2.0 (ụdị: "Ngwa Weebụ")
> 3. Tinye URL ihe nkesa gị dị ka URI ntụgharị ikike
> 4. Dochie ụkpụrụ asambodo na `.env`.
