## 12. Ntọgharị User-Agent nke Ndị Na-eweta Ọrụ

Gbanwee nkụnye `User-Agent` a na-ezigara onye na-eweta ọrụ ọ bụla dị n'elu. Klas ntọala executor na-ekpebi nke a n'ụzọ na-agbanwe agbanwe n'oge ọ na-arụ ọrụ:

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Agbanwe | Uru Ndabara | Mgbe A Ga-emelite Ya |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.258 (external, cli)` | Mgbe Anthropic wepụtara ụdị CLI ọhụrụ |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`/`true` iji zipụ aha ngwaọrụ third-party harness n'ụzọ ha dị kpọmkwem na Anthropic n'ọnọdụ ụzọ abụọ na-aga Anthropic (OAuth nke ya na CLIProxyAPI). Na ndabara, executor na-enye aha ọzọ a na-ekpebi mgbe niile nye aha ngwaọrụ ndị na-abụghị nke Claude Code (iji nhazi aha izizi nke Claude Code ebe nke dị, ma ọ bụghị ya jiri PascalCase), ma tụgharịa ha azụ na nzaghachi site na `_toolNameMap`, ka a ghara ịjụ harness nwere ngwaọrụ snake_case dịka ndị ahịa third-party a matara site na njirimara ha. Maka debugging naanị. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.155.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Mgbe OpenAI melitere Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.155.0` | Gbanwee ụdị Codex client n'adabereghị na eriri UA zuru ezu |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.258` | Gbanwee ụdị Claude Code a na-akpọsa n'adabereghị na `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT`. Anthropic na-egbochi ụfọdụ model dabere na uru a (#12417). |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.81-6` | Gbanwee ụdị Copilot CLI a na-akpọsa n'adabereghị na `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Mgbe GitHub Copilot Chat melitere |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_USER_AGENT` | `antigravity/2.0.1 darwin/arm64` | Mgbe Antigravity IDE melitere |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Mgbe Kiro IDE melitere |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Gbanwee `clientId` (id ọha) nke Kiro social device-code |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Nhọrọ ịbanye: nkwado CRC zuru ezu maka frame ọ bụla na Kiro event stream (iji debug stream mebiri emebi) |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Mgbe Qoder CLI melitere |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Mgbe Cursor melitere |

> [!TIP]
> Ị nwere ike ịgbakwunye ntọgharị User-Agent maka onye na-eweta ọrụ **ọ bụla** site n'iji ụkpụrụ `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Executor na-ewulite aha env var ahụ n'ụzọ na-agbanwe agbanwe.

---

## 13. Ndakọrịta Akara-Mkpịsịaka CLI

Mgbe agbanyere ya, OmniRoute na-ahazigharị isiokwu HTTP na mpaghara ahụ JSON ka ha kwekọọ kpọmkwem na akara njirimara nke ngwa CLI gọọmentị. Nke a na-ebelata ihe ize ndụ nke ịka akaụntụ gị akara ma na-echekwa IP proxy gị.

**Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Maka Onye Na-enye Ọrụ Ọ Bụla

| Mgbanwe                  | Mgbalite | Mmetụta                              |
| ------------------------ | -------- | ------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Codex CLI      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Claude Code    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ GitHub Copilot |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Antigravity    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Cursor         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Kimi Coding    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Kilo Code      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Cline          |

### Nke Izugbe

| Mgbanwe          | Mgbalite | Mmetụta                                                                 |
| ---------------- | -------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL` | `=1`     | Gbaa ndakọrịta akara-mkpịsịaka maka ndị na-enye ọrụ **niile** otu mgbe. |

### Mgbanwe njirimara Kimi Coding CLI

| Mgbanwe                 | Nke ndabara          | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                        | Nkọwa                                                            |
| ----------------------- | -------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`             | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Dochie ụdị Kimi CLI a na-eziga n'oge oku OAuth/API.              |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(ndabara e jidere)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Dochie ID ngwaọrụ Kimi e jidere nke a na-eji n'isiokwu klayenti. |

> [!NOTE]
> Njirimara a na-arụkọ ọrụ na mgbanwe User-Agent (§12). Sistemụ akara-mkpịsịaka na-ahụ maka nhazi isiokwu na nhazi mpaghara ahụ, ebe mgbanwe User-Agent na-ahụ maka eriri UA akọwapụtara. Enwere ike ịgbanye ha abụọ n'adabereghị onwe ha.

---

## 14. Ndị Na-enye Ọrụ Igodo API

Igodo API maka ndị na-enye ọrụ na-eji nyocha njirimara ozugbo. **Nhazi akwadoro:** Dashboard → Providers → Add API Key.

Ịtọ ha site na mgbanwe gburugburu bụ ụzọ ọzọ maka mbugharị Docker ma ọ bụ mbugharị na-enweghị ihu njikwa.

Ụkpụrụ a na-amata: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Mgbanwe            | Onye na-enye ọrụ                                     |
| ------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                             |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                           |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Foundation API + Reader ndabere)            |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (aha ọzọ maka `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)             |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) embeddings + ndabere nkata |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (aha ọzọ maka `GEMINI_API_KEY`)               |

> [!NOTE]
> E wepụrụ ndenye `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` kwụ ọtọ maka Groq, xAI, Mistral, Perplexity, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius, na Qianfan na v3.8.0 n'ihi na runtime anaghịzi agụ ha — ndị na-enye ọrụ ahụ na-adabere naanị na Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB ezoro ezo. Hụ ngalaba _Nnyocha: Mgbanwe E Wepụrụ / Ndị Na-adịghị Arụ Ọrụ_ dị na njedebe akwụkwọ a maka ụzọ mbugharị.

> [!TIP]
> A na-echekwa igodo ndị edobere site na Dashboard n'ụdị ezoro ezo na SQLite, ha na-ebutekwa mgbanwe gburugburu ụzọ.
>
> **Jina:** embeddings `jina-ai/…`, rerank, classify, segment, na `jina-search` anaghị akwụ ụgwọ igodo env klọsta mgbe njikọ dashboard `jina-ai` (ma ọ bụ `jina-reader` a na-ekekọrịta) dị — `getProviderCredentials` na-ebu ụzọ mejupụta. A na-eji `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` naanị mgbe enweghị igodo dashboard a pụrụ iji. Ndekọ oku na-egosi ndabere env dịka `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Kaadị Reader (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) anaghị enye `/v1/embeddings` ma ọ bụ `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (aha ọzọ `google/gemini-embedding-2`) na-ebu ụzọ jiri njikọ dashboard `gemini`. A na-eji `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` naanị mgbe enweghị igodo dashboard a pụrụ iji. Ndekọ oku na-egosi ndabere env dịka `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Okporo data multimodal nke izizi na-eji `x-goog-api-key` megide `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` — ihe N OpenAI `input` na-aghọ vector N.

---

## 15. Ntọala Oge Nchere

Uru niile dị na **milisekọnd**. Mkpebi e mere n'otu ebe dị na `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Usoro Ndị Oge Nchere

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (mgbanwe zuru ụwa ọnụ)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (oku ndị na-eweta ọrụ dị n'elu, ndabara: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (nọọrọ onwe ya, ndabara: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (nọọrọ onwe ya, ndabara: 15000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS (nọọrọ onwe ya, ndabara: 0 = agbanyụrụ, nke a tụrụ aro: 30000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS (nọọrọ onwe ya, ndabara: 2)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (nọọrọ onwe ya, ndabara: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (nọọrọ onwe ya, ndabara: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (nọọrọ onwe ya, ndabara: 1260000; 0 na-agbanyụ ya)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (na-amachi mgbatị oge njikere na-agbanwe agbanwe, ndabara: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (na-eketa site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (e sitere na ya nweta, ndabara: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 0 = agbanyụrụ)
```

| Agbanwe                                         | Ndabara                                                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                            | _(edobeghị)_                                                      | Ụzọ mkpirisi zuru ụwa ọnụ — na-akagbu ndabara nke ma `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` ma `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | `600000`                                                          | Ngụkọta oge njedebe nke arịrịọ HTTP maka oku ndị na-aga na ndị na-eweta ọrụ dị n'elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `600000`                                                          | Oge juu kacha ogologo n'etiti baitị raw ndị si n'elu tupu a kwụsị ya. Model ndị na-eche echiche ogologo oge anaghị akwụsịkarị karịa sekọnd 90.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `1260000`                                                         | Ngụkọta oge ndụ kachasị ogologo nke iyi SSE na-arụ ọrụ; ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-amalitegharị n'ihi baitị ndị si n'elu, ọ dabereghịkwa na `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. E sitere na `timeoutMs` kachasị ukwuu nke model ọ bụla dị na ndekọ ahụ (1200000, Codex), tinyere oke mgbakwunye 60000, ka e wee ghara igbu model e nyere ohere iji oge ya niile mgbe ọ ka na-aza. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                        | _(agbanyụrụ)_                                                     | Ma OmniRoute nwere ike izipu ahịrị nkọwa SSE `:` (dịka obi-ọkụ `: keepalive` na metadata trailer `x-omniroute-*`). A na-agbanyụ ya na ndabara (#10524) n'ihi na client ndị na-agbasochi ndakọrịta OpenAI anya na-eme JSON.parse n'ahịrị SSE ọ bụla ma daa mgbe ha hụrụ nkọwa `:`; nke a anaghị emetụta obi-ọkụ `data:`. Tọọ `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` iji họrọ ịlaghachi na ya. `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `80000`                                                           | Oge e ji echere ịnata event SSE mbụ na-abụghị ping. Ọ na-eketa `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` mgbe edobere ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `180000`                                                          | Oge njikere mgbanwe kachasị ogologo maka event mbụ nke arịrịọ streaming buru ibu, ndị ji tool nke ukwuu, ma ọ bụ ndị chọrọ nnukwu echiche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_POLICY_ENABLED`           | `true`                                                            | Mgbanwe mberede iji kwụsị heuristic `/goal`. Tọọ `false`/`0`/`off` iji gbanyụọ nchọpụta kpamkpam — a gaghị abawanye oge njedebe njikere na mgbake iyi n'ihi body/header nke arịrịọ, nke na-ebelata mmụba oge njedebe client na-achịkwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000`                                                          | Oge njikere kachasị ogologo maka event mbụ nke agent run `/goal` achọpụtara ma ọ bụ arịrịọ e ji `x-omniroute-agent-goal` manye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_STREAM_RECOVERY`          | `true`                                                            | Mee ka mgbake iyi n'oge na-amalite na-akpaghị aka maka agent run `/goal` achọpụtara. Tọọ `false`/`0`/`off` iji gbanyụọ nhọrọ nbanye pụrụ iche maka goal. Nke a nwere ike naanị ỊTỤKWASỊ mgbake n'elu ndabara onye njikwa — ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-akagbu nhọrọ ọpụpụ e mere n'ụzọ doro anya site na ntọala `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`       | `true`                                                            | Wepụ event SSE `codex.*` na-abụghị ọkọlọtọ (dịka `codex.rate_limits`) nke na-eme ka `responses.stream()` nke OpenAI SDK daa na 502. Ọ dị ON na ndabara (#11014). Tọọ `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` iji zipụ ha gaa n'ihu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                  | _(edobeghị)_                                                      | Transport app-server Codex a ga-ahọrọ ịbanye na ya. Endpoint WebSocket (`ws://`/`wss://`) nke sidecar `codex app-server` mpaghara. Mgbe e tinyere ya na token, a na-ebufe arịrịọ Codex site na JSON-RPC gaa na sidecar kama iji HTTP Responses API. A pụkwara ịtọ ya maka connection ọ bụla site na `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerUrl`. `open-sse/executors/codex/appServerConfig.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`            | _(edobeghị)_                                                      | Token ikike/bearer dị n'ime a na-enye app-server. Nkáagbu maka connection ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN_FILE`       | _(edobeghị)_                                                      | Path gaa na faịlụ nwere token ikike app-server (sitere na `codex app-server --ws-token-file`). A na-eji ya mgbe edobeghị `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`. Nkáagbu maka connection ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerTokenFile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_CWD`                 | `/tmp`                                                            | Directory ọrụ ebe turn app-server na-arụ. Nkáagbu maka connection ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerCwd`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_APPROVAL`            | _(edobeghị)_                                                      | Iwu nkwado a na-enyefe na turn app-server (dịka `never`, `on-request`). Nkáagbu maka connection ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerApprovalPolicy`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_SANDBOX`             | _(edobeghị)_                                                      | Iwu sandbox a na-enyefe na turn app-server (dịka `read-only`, `workspace-write`, `danger-full-access`). Mgbe edobeghị ya, executor na-eji `workspace-write` dịka ndabara (e wusiri nchekwa ya ike; ọ bụbu `danger-full-access`). Nkáagbu maka connection ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerSandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_AUTO_APPROVE`        | `false`                                                           | Kwado ozugbo prompt nkwado nke app-server n'onwe ya (ime command/faịlụ/ikike na host). A na-agbanyụ ya na ndabara — a na-ajụ prompt na-akpaghị aka; nke a anaghị emetụta oku tool harness (ha na-agafe n'ụzọ passthrough `item/tool/call` dị iche). Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`yes`. Nkáagbu maka connection ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge e ji echere ịnata header nzaghachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000` (30s)                                                     | Oge nchere kachasị (ms) tupu nzaghachi amalite maka mbọ kpọmkwem ọ bụla na-enweghị proxy. Mgbe oge gwụrụ, a na-anwaleghachi otu ugboro site na socket ọhụrụ; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ oke a ma debe omume gara aga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_RESPONSE_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS`    | `600000` (nkeji 10)                                               | Oke kachasị (ms) maka mbọ RETRY site na socket ọhụrụ mgbe oge nchere mmalite nzaghachi nke mbọ jiri njikọ echekwara n'ọdọ gwụrụ dịka akọwara n'elu (#13703). Ọ na-emetụta naanị mgbe onye kpọrọ ọrụ ahụ etinyelarị akara ngwụcha oge nke ya (usoro njikọ/model/provider/`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` a kpebiri); akara ahụ bụ ezigbo oke ma na-arụ ọrụ mbụ n'ụzọ e bu n'obi, ya mere nke a bụ nchekwa ndabere sara mbara kama ịbụ oke kwụ ọtọ — ma e wepụ ya, nnwale ọzọ ga-eji otu windo dị mkpụmkpụ `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS` ahụ mbọ sitere n'ọdọ jiri, wee nye model echiche ndị dị mma ma nwee TTFB dị nwayọ njehie 504. A naghị ekwe ka ọ dị n'okpuru oke ala kwụ ọtọ dị n'elu; mgbe onye kpọrọ ọrụ enyeghị akara ngwụcha oge ọ bụla, nnwale ọzọ na-ahapụ oke ala kwụ ọtọ ahụ ka ọ dị. |
| `FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge iji nata ahụ nzaghachi dum.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `30000`                                                           | Oge ngwụcha maka ịmepụta njikọ TCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                    | `4000`                                                            | Oge ngwụcha nke socket keep-alive mgbe ọ naghị arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge ngwụcha nke proxy akara-mkpịsịaka TLS (wreq-js).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS`                    | `10000`                                                           | Na-amachi oge ruo mgbe byte mbụ rutere kpọmkwem n'ahụ ozi nke usoro mbufe akara-mkpịsịaka TLS nke wreq-js; naanị `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS` enweghị ike ịchọpụta ahụ ozi kwụsịrị ebe ọ na-ewere na ọrụ agwụla ozugbo headers rutere (#12656). Mgbe oge gwụrụ, ọ na-akagbu wreq reader ma laghachi na direct/proxy dispatcher; `0` na-agbanyụọ watchdog ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `15000`                                                           | Maka OpenCode executor naanị, na naanị mgbe feature flag `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_STALL_ROTATION` dị ọkụ (ọ na-adị ọkụghị na ndabara): na-amachi oge nchere maka byte mbụ nke ahụ ozi na nzaghachi Responses a na-eziga dịka stream mgbe headers ya rutere (#13484). Stream Responses na-amalite na `response.created`, ya mere ịgbachi nkịtị gafee windo a pụtara na ọ kwụsịrị: a na-eme ka akaụntụ ahụ zuru ike ma tụgharịa arịrịọ ahụ gaa na akaụntụ ọzọ otu ugboro; nkwụsị nke abụọ na-eme ka ọ daa ozugbo. `0` na-agbanyụọ nche ahụ ọbụlagodi mgbe flag dị ọkụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS`            | `0` (= agbanyụrụ)                                                 | Maka OpenCode executor naanị: na-amachi oge nchere maka headers nzaghachi upstream na oku Responses a na-eziga dịka stream mgbe akaụntụ ọzọ ka dị — arịrịọ dị n'ahịrị na-akwụsị ichere wee gaa n'ihu kama ijide windo headers dum. Ọ na-emetụta naanị oku Responses a na-eziga dịka stream (stream Responses na-amalite na `response.created`, ya mere ịgbachi nkịtị n'ebe ahụ pụtara ahịrị nchere, ọ bụghị mmepụta); chat completions na oku ndị anaghị eji stream anaghị agbanwe, windo a na-ebelata naanị oke oge dị irè maka mmalite fetch, ọ naghị agbatị ya. Uru a tụrụ aro mgbe a na-agbanye ya: `30000`. `0` na-agbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS` | `2`                                                               | Maka OpenCode executor naanị: ugboro ole oke nchere headers dị n'elu nwere ike ibugharị arịrịọ ọ bụla gaa na akaụntụ ọzọ. Akaụntụ ikpeazụ fọdụrụ na-edobe windo headers dum mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_AND_RESUME`                      | `false`                                                           | Maka OpenCode executor naanị: debe arịrịọ ahụ nwa oge site na heartbeat mgbe njehie 429 ndị na-adịru nwa oge mere ugboro ugboro (ma ọ bụ mgbe e nwere akara pool-strain ọhụrụ), wee kpọgharịa otu akụkụ nwere oke nke ruru akaụntụ 3 n'usoro kama iziga ya n'ụgbọ niile n'otu oge (#13924). Ọ na-adị ọkụghị na ndabara: njehie 429 ọ bụla na-atụgharị gaa na akaụntụ ọzọ otu ahụ ọ dị na mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `STREAM_READINESS_STALL_RETRY`                  | `false`                                                           | Maka chat a na-eziga dịka stream naanị: mgbe ahụ ozi upstream mbụ kwụsịrị tupu o mepụta event a pụrụ iji, mee mbọ nke abụọ nwere oke site n'otu ụzọ routing ahụ, jiri otu readiness budget ahụ, na-enweghị ntaramahụhụ akaụntụ. Ọ na-adị ọkụghị na ndabara: ahụ ozi mbụ kwụsịrị na-eme ka arịrịọ daa n'enweghị nnwale ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_RESELECT`                        | `false`                                                           | Maka OpenCode executor naanị: mgbe njehie 429 sitere na provider e kenyere n'òtù egress na akaụntụ na-enweghị proxy n'okpuru ọnọdụ pool gbara ya gburugburu, rịọ connection pool ka o nye member ọzọ maka mbọ na-esote kama ịnwaleghachi otu adreesị egress ahụ. Ọ na-ahazi usoro, ọ naghị ewepụ: pool gwụchara na-edobe omume dị ugbu a. Ọ na-adị ọkụghị na ndabara: njehie 429 ọ bụla na-atụgharị gaa na akaụntụ ọzọ otu ahụ ọ dị na mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_STRAIN_MARKER_PATH`              | _(edoghị)_                                                        | Maka OpenCode executor naanị: dochie path nke akara pool-strain a na-agụ tupu idobe arịrịọ nwa oge (`{since, reason, ttl_s}`, ndabara `/tmp/opencode-pool-strain.json`, #13924). Akara ọhụrụ na-edobe arịrịọ nwa oge n'agụtaghị ọzọ; ma ọ bụrụ na ọ dịghị ma ọ bụ na ọ kara nká, a na-alaghachi na burst counter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `30000`                                                           | Oge ngwụcha nke nzọụkwụ proxy maka arịrịọ bridge `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `FIRECRAWL_BASE_URL`                            | `https://api.firecrawl.dev`                                       | Tụnye Firecrawl web-fetch executor gaa na instance nke ị na-akwado n'onwe gị (API key abụghị iwu ma ọ bụrụ na ọ bụghị na cloud).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `FIRECRAWL_TIMEOUT_MS`                          | `30000`                                                           | Oge ngwụcha maka arịrịọ ọ bụla nke Firecrawl web-fetch executor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `TAVILY_BASE_URL`                               | _(edoghị ya)_                                                     | URL ntọala maka onye na-eweta oke `/usage` nke Tavily (Oke Ndị Na-enye Ọrụ); a na-eleghara ya anya mgbe ọ na-atụ aka na `api.tavily.com`. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, a na-eji URL ntọala nke njikọ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `300000`                                                          | Oge nchere zuru ezu maka arịrịọ sava nke àkwà mmiri ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                                                           | Oge e nyere iji zipụ nkụnyeisi nzaghachi site n'àkwà mmiri ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`        | `5000`                                                            | Oge nchere mgbe njikọ keep-alive nke àkwà mmiri ahụ anaghị arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `0`                                                               | Oge nchere socket ozugbo (0 = agbanyụrụ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS`                           | `30000`                                                           | Oge amara mgbe SIGTERM/SIGINT gasịrị tupu mmemme ahụ apụ n'ike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEFAULT_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `120000`                                                          | Uru ndabere nke `src/shared/utils/fetchTimeout.ts` na-eji mgbe edoghị `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `8000`                                                            | Oge nchere (ms) maka nhazi ndị `validationRead` na `modelsProbe` dị na `src/shared/network/safeOutboundFetch.ts`. Welie ya maka njedebe njikọ ndị dị nwayọọ (Cerebras, Cloudflare AI, Groq) iji gbochie ịgbanwegharị n'etiti active/error na dashboard. Ọ na-alaghachi na 8000ms maka uru na-ezighi ezi (<1000) ma ọ bụ uru na-abụghị ọnụọgụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`              | `25000`                                                           | Oge nchere fetch pụrụ iche maka relay dị na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). Relay kwụsịrị ịzaghachi ga-ada tupu oge nchere nke client/agent (~30s) ka ndị kpọrọ ya hụ ọdịda pụrụ iche nke relay kama oge nchere upstream izugbe. E debere oke ya na `29000` ka ọ na-ebute ụzọ mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS`                    | `10`                                                              | Oge nkwụsịkọ ọnụ tupu ịnwalegharị maka ụzọ direct/relay/proxy ndị na-anwalegharị otu ugboro dị na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). `0` = nwaleegharị ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                                                           | Oge nchere arịrịọ wreq-js nke ala (`claudeTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Oge amara maka oke ikpeazụ JS zuru oke nke agbakwunyere n'elu oge nchere nke ala.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `30000`                                                           | Oge nchere arịrịọ wreq-js nke ala (`perplexityTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Oge amara maka oke ikpeazụ JS zuru oke nke agbakwunyere n'elu oge nchere nke ala.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_SEARCH_HINT`                    | `0` (agbanyụrụ)                                                   | Na-agbakwunye "Ị nwere nchọta webụ e wuru n'ime ya. Zaa ajụjụ ozugbo site n'iji nsonaazụ nchọta." na ozi sistemụ nke onye kpọrọ ya (`perplexity-web/protocol.ts`). Agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara — Perplexity na-achọ ihe n'agbanyeghị nke ahụ, ahịrịokwu ahụ na-apụtakwa na nzaghachi dịka nkọwa gbasara nzaghachi n'onwe ya maka ndị client na-ede koodu. Tọọ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji weghachite ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `60000`                                                           | Oge nchere arịrịọ wreq-js nke ala (`grokTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Oge amara maka oke ikpeazụ JS zuru oke nke agbakwunyere n'elu oge nchere nke ala.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                                                           | Oge nchere arịrịọ wreq-js nke ala (`notionTlsClient.ts`); `notion-web` na-ebuli ya kwa arịrịọ ruo `180000` maka mmepụta ogologo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Oge amara maka oke ikpeazụ JS zuru oke nke agbakwunyere n'elu oge nchere nke ala.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_BROWSER_POOL`                        | `on`                                                              | Ọdọ browser Playwright ekekọrịtara maka nkata web-cookie nke browser na-akwado (`browserPool.ts`); tọọ `off` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OBSCURA_BIN`                                   | `auto-detect`                                                     | Ụzọ faịlụ binary `obscura` nke ọdọ browser na onye mmebe Cloudflare Playground na-eji dịka injin mbụ (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`); a na-achọpụta ya na-akpaghị aka site na PATH nke sistemụ mgbe edoghị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                          | _(edoghị ya)_                                                     | Tụnye ya na Obscura na-arụ ọrụ ugbua (`http://host:port`) kama ịmalite nke ọhụrụ; modul ahụ anaghị achịkwa usoro ọrụ ahụ (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                  | `random free port`                                                | Port akọwapụtara maka `obscura serve` a malitere; a na-ahọrọ port efu na-akpaghị aka mgbe edoghị ya (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                        | `0`                                                               | Họrọ ka arịrịọ nkata web-cookie jiri ụzọ browser na-akwado (`browserBackedChat.ts`); `1` iji gbanye ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                             | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | URL ntọala maka onye mmebe iwu Kimi Web (kimi.ai Connect-RPC nke mba ụwa) (`kimi-web.ts`); gbanwee ya naanị maka endpoint enyo/proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                             | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Endpoint nkata zuru ezu maka onye mmebe iwu Kimi Web (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | _(achọpụtara na-akpaghị aka)_                                     | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ Chrome/Edge nke sistemụ nwere ike ịrụ ọrụ maka nbanye ihe nchọgharị mmekọrịta nke Adobe Firefly (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); ọ na-edochi nchọpụta akpaghị aka nke OS ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                      | _mmepụta standalone nke .build/_                                  | Mgbanwe oge-build maka ndekọ mmepụta standalone nke usoro idokọta ihe ọnụ mgbe build gachara na-eji (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); ọ bụ maka ngwa build, ọ bụghị runtime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

Mgbalị combo target na-eketa oge nchere arịrịọ upstream edoziworo (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, ma ọ bụ
`REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` mgbe ọ na-enye ndabara fetch). Tọọ `targetTimeoutMs` n'ime combo,
ndabara combo, ma ọ bụ mgbanwe provider naanị iji mee ka fallback combo dị ngwa; a na-amachi ụkpụrụ ndị karịrị
oge nchere upstream dị ugbu a ka ha ruo naanị oge nchere upstream ahụ.

`comboTimeoutMs` bụ mmefu oge wall-clock dị iche maka combo dum n'ofe target failover.
Hapụ ya ka a ghara ịtọ ya ma ọ bụ tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji nọgide na-eme iteration na-enweghị oke (ihe nkwụsị hang nke nkeji 10 e dere kpọmkwem n'ime
`comboPredicates.ts` ka na-emetụta ya). Uru dị mma na-anọchi
ụgbụ nchekwa ahụ maka combo. Mee ka `comboTimeoutMs` dị ogologo karịa `targetTimeoutMs` ka failover
ka nwee oge mgbe target mbụ jiri nwayọ.

### Ọnụ Ọgụgụ Mgbalite Circuit Breaker

Nhazi circuit breaker n'ọkwa provider. Ndabara na-egosipụta ụkpụrụ agbasawanyere ejirila kemgbe v3.6 maka njikọ 500+.

| Mgbanwe                                                       | Ndabara   | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite              | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_THRESHOLD`                   | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Oke ọdịda ndị na-esochi onwe ha maka ndị na-eweta OAuth tupu ihe nkwụsị sekit ahụ emeghe.                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_RESET_MS`                    | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Oge windo nrụgharị (ms) maka ihe nkwụsị sekit nke onye na-eweta OAuth.                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_THRESHOLD`                 | `12`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Oke ọdịda ndị na-esochi onwe ha maka ndị na-eweta API-key.                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_RESET_MS`                  | `30000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Oge windo nrụgharị (ms) maka ihe nkwụsị sekit nke onye na-eweta API-key.                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_THRESHOLD`                   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Oke ọdịda ndị na-esochi onwe ha maka ndị na-eweta mpaghara (Ollama, LM Studio, ...).                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_RESET_MS`                    | `15000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Oge windo nrụgharị (ms) maka ihe nkwụsị sekit nke onye na-eweta mpaghara.                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `10`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ihe nkwụsị n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: ọnụọgụ ọdịda n'ime windo tupu onye na-eweta OAuth dum abanye n'oge izuike.                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `900000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ihe nkwụsị n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: windo na-aga n'ihu maka ịgụ ọdịda (ms) nke ndị na-eweta OAuth.                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ihe nkwụsị n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: oge izuike (ms) ozugbo e ruru oke onye na-eweta OAuth.                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`      | `5`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Onye na-eweta OAuth na-abanye na DEGRADED mgbe ọdịda ruru ọnụọgụ a.                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`     | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ọnụọgụ kachasị eji amụba nrịgo resetTimeout nke onye na-eweta OAuth.                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT`   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Onye na-eweta OAuth na-ebuli ọkwa mgbe okirikiri mmeghe ruru ọnụọgụ a.                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`        | `15`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ihe nkwụsị n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: ọnụọgụ ọdịda n'ime windo tupu onye na-eweta API-key dum abanye n'oge izuike.                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`        | `1800000` | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ihe nkwụsị n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: windo na-aga n'ihu maka ịgụ ọdịda (ms) nke ndị na-eweta API-key.                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`              | `600000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ihe nkwụsị n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: oge izuike (ms) ozugbo e ruru oke onye na-eweta API-key.                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`    | `7`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Onye na-eweta API-key na-abanye na DEGRADED mgbe ọdịda ruru ọnụọgụ a.                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`   | `4`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ọnụọgụ kachasị eji amụba nrịgo resetTimeout nke onye na-eweta API-key.                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT` | `3`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Onye na-eweta API-key na-ebuli ọkwa mgbe okirikiri mmeghe ruru ọnụọgụ a.                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ihe nkwụsị n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: ọnụọgụ ọdịda tupu onye na-eweta mpaghara dum abanye n'oge izuike.                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ihe nkwụsị n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: windo na-aga n'ihu maka ịgụ ọdịda (ms) nke ndị na-eweta mpaghara.                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Ihe nkwụsị n'ọkwa onye na-eweta: oge izuike (ms) ozugbo e ruru oke onye na-eweta mpaghara.                                                                   |
| `PIN_DROP_BACKOFF_LEVEL`                                      | `2`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`   | Omimi ndọghachi azụ nke a na-ewere onye na-eweta pin nke context-cache dị ka onye na-adịghị arụ ọrụ nke ọma ruo ogologo oge, wee hapụ pin ahụ maka failover. |
| `PIN_DROP_GRACE_MS`                                           | `20000`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`   | Windo mgbochi ịma jijiji (ms) nke na-anabata obere oge izuike na-adịteghị aka tupu a hapụ pin nke context-cache.                                             |

### Ọnọdụ

| Ọnọdụ                                  | Nhazi                                                     |
| -------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Mmepụta koodu na-ewe ogologo oge**   | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (nkeji 15)                    |
| **Kpagide ngụkọta oge iyi ahụ ga-adị** | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (nkeji 21)             |
| **Ọdịda ngwa ngwa maka API mmepụta**   | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                       |
| **Ụdịdị nwere echiche gbatịrị agbatị** | `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000` (nkeji 5 n'etiti iberibe) |
